# Plan review record — f-20261101-13 quit gate for unsaved tabs

* **Finding:** f-20261101-13 (frontend-state, entry build, impact data-loss) — "Quitting the app discards every unsaved tab without a prompt".
* **Plan:** `tasks/plans/2026-11-01-quit-gate-unsaved-tabs.md` in the planner's worktree; adopted by the drain build run (session 5760d80b-dd1f-45c3-80d3-ecf9daae3008) as `tasks/plans/2026-11-02-quit-gate.md`. `tasks/plans/` is gitignored, so neither path is in the tree; this record and the decisions d-20261102-05..08 carry the plan's content. Planned PLAN-ONLY at BASE `095048137821e5d7a03c6ae797f7e12153c0fa71` (r1-r5, approved), refreshed PLAN-REFRESH to NEW-BASE `7cea140f66fbccf50fc0cce0b2556f0cd9ca9636` (r6-r8, approved). Drain session 147ad331-3648-4803-ad16-da0f3d8861ff.
* **Orchestrator:** Claude Code (Opus 5.5). **Lens executor:** Grok via `leaf-launch.sh` read-only leaves (`review-plan` role for review-plan, `sensitive` for the others); every profile check exited 0. Family separation from the orchestrator held for every lens.
* **Outcome:** 9 rounds (r9 a publisher-requested closure round for the carried section); 43 unique issues (I1-I43), all Fix: 42 closed at plan level, 1 carried to diff review (CR-1, I43); 0 Skip, 0 Defer, 0 withdrawn, 0 open; no out-of-area or out-of-drift finding filed.
* **Adoptions per round (parsed findings):** r1=28 r2=13 r3=5 r4=7 r5=0 r6=17 r7=3 r8=1 r9=0. **Wall per round (s):** r1=508 r2=461 r3=494 r4=392 r5=190 r6=428 r7=374 r8=290 r9=166.
* **Round-1 lens set:** every plan-capable lens: plan, minimalism, correctness, error-handling, tests, chess-semantics, engine-protocol, ipc-contract, persisted-state, pgn-index, platform-semantics, tauri-security (code-quality and root-cause are `plan-review: false`). chess-semantics, pgn-index and tauri-security answered NOT APPLICABLE with reasons the arbiter accepted; engine-protocol and platform-semantics approved with no findings.
* **Refresh:** r6 ran exactly the 12 REFRESH-LENSES over the drift file `drift-095048137821…-7cea140f66fb….diff` and the orchestrator's drift correction RF1. The drift fixed f-20261101-14 and recorded `d-20261101-13` (which tabs prompt on close), so the quit gate now reuses that coverage rule through one extracted predicate in `src/utils/tabs.ts` that `closeTab` and the gate share (rule 11), instead of blocking on every dirty analysis/play tree. Closure rounds r7 (plan, correctness, error-handling, minimalism, persisted-state, tests) and r8 (plan, tests, minimalism).
* **Unavailable evidence:** the MANDATE's real-app probe (trees do not survive a restart) cannot run in planner mode; it is a mandatory Phase 2 `pnpm verify:app` assertion, and the plan stops for replanning if it shows trees survive.
* **Limitation:** the Grok leaf sandbox could not open `.claude/skills/verify-ui/SKILL.md` (r5 review-plan note); the verify:app obligations rest on `scripts/verify-app.mjs`, which the lenses read.
* **Load before implementing or reviewing:** this record and the plan; diff review must close CR-1.

## Carried to diff review

* **CR-1** (I43, Fix — carried to CR-1): when Phase 1 gives the persistent close cases in
  `src/components/tabs/BoardsPage.test.tsx` a real persistent `gameOrigin` (I40), the file's
  `TabFixture` type (`BoardsPage.test.tsx:70-71`, `gameOrigin: { kind: "none" }`) is widened in the
  same file so those fixtures type-check. Close by the cumulative diff review: `pnpm lint`
  (`tsgo --noEmit`) green and the widened fixture visible in the diff.

## Decided autonomously

Decided autonomously (full auto):

* **D1 — One gate function, two entry points:** the close-request listener and the menu actions
  both call the same gate. Rejected: routing File → Exit and macOS Quit through `window.close()`
  so the listener is the only entry (r1 version) — the menu paths would then be ungated whenever
  listener registration failed (r1 review, I12). Rejected: a Rust-side
  `CloseRequested`/`ExitRequested` prevent that asks the renderer over a new IPC event (a new event
  and round trip for a decision only the renderer can make). Reversal: route the menu actions back
  to `exit(0)` and keep the listener.
* **D2 — Tab states that need a decision:** the tabs `d-20261101-13` covers for tab close
  (persistent origin, or type `analysis` of any origin), through one shared coverage predicate,
  block when dirty or in freshness `conflict`/`unavailable`; any `play`, `analysis` or `puzzles` tab
  blocks while its tree is uninspectable, the same set `closeTab` refuses to close
  (`BoardsPage.tsx:124-132`); new tabs never block, and a dirty origin-`none` play or puzzle tab
  does not block. Rejected: blocking on every dirty `analysis`/`play` tree (the r1-r5
  version of this plan) — `d-20261101-13`, recorded after that version, rejected prompting on every
  finished engine game, and the same tab must not be "unsaved" on quit but "nothing to save" on
  close. Rejected: a quit-only copy of the rule — a second implementation of the same concept
  (rule 11). Rejected: ignoring uninspectable trees — "could not check" is not "saved" under a
  data-loss impact. Reversal: the shared predicate in `src/utils/tabs.ts` (both callers follow) and
  the quit-only arms in `src/state/quitGate.ts`.
* **D3 — Summary dialog, not per-tab `ConfirmChangesModal`:** one dialog with Cancel / Quit without
  saving. Rejected: chaining `ConfirmChangesModal` per dirty tab — it is bound to closing one tab
  (its `onSaved` closes the tab, its copy says "before closing"), n tabs means n dialogs, and
  origin-`none`/draft saves need their own picker flows mid-quit. Saving stays on the normal per-tab
  save surfaces after Cancel. Precedent: the existing unsaved-changes prompt on tab close
  (`BoardsPage.tsx:133-141`, `Tab.UnsavedChanges`) — the same promise extended to quitting, so this is
  not a novel product question. Reversal: add a "Save all" action later without changing the gate.
* **D4 — Fail closed with a way out:** every keep-alive path prevents the close, a scan error still
  shows the dialog with both buttons, and a failed destroy falls back to `exit(0)`. Rejected: fail
  open (silent data loss on a bug); rejected: block quit entirely (an app that cannot be closed).

verification (window.js:1632-1640, useTauriListener.ts:37-45, tree.ts:147,237-243,821-822,

verify-app.mjs:3249-3321). Adopted at r1: 28 findings → 20 issues.

* **I1** keep-alive paths must `preventDefault()` on every event before any await (second close, scan throw, gate exception) — witnesses correctness#1, error-handling#1, ipc-contract#1, ipc-contract#5 — MANDATE "the app quits at once and the edits are gone" — Fix: O1 handler prevents synchronously, gate destroys itself.
* **I2** `useTauriListener` cannot carry the close listener (fire-and-forget callback) — ipc-contract#2 — Fix: O1 names the awaited-handler requirement.
* **I3** File → Exit change breaks `e2e/async-errors.spec.ts` consumers outside Phase 2 proof — plan#1, ipc-contract#4 — Fix: Phase 2 files/proof include async-errors, assertions move to `plugin:window|destroy`.
* **I4** Cancel from a non-board route leaves the user off the save surfaces — plan#2 — Fix: Cancel navigates to `/` and activates the first listed tab.
* **I5** Phase 1 proof would not run the dialog test — plan#3, tests#3 — Fix: dialog pinned to `src/components/tabs/QuitConfirmModal.tsx`, proof paths cover `src/state`.
* **I6** dirty `verify:app` probe would break later titlebar closes — plan#4 — Fix: own session or last close of its process.
* **I7** shared-predicate justification is a seam for a future caller; sibling-module option unpinned — minimalism#1 — Fix: quit-only predicate pinned in `src/state/quitGate.ts`, future-caller justification removed.
* **I8** full cartesian unit matrix — minimalism#2 (nit) — Fix: distinguishing cases only (arbiter-closed nit).
* **I9** scan reader: cached store authoritative, never `closeTreeStore`/`createTreeStore`, read at tree key — correctness#2, persisted-state#1 — Fix: O2 reader contract.
* **I10** fail-closed Cancel has no tab to activate — correctness#3 — Fix: fail-closed Cancel keeps route and tab.
* **I11** Playwright fixture must emit `tauri://close-requested` on `close` and record `destroy` — correctness#4, tests#4, ipc-contract#3 — Fix: mandatory fixture pair in O5.
* **I12** listener registration failure leaves menu quits ungated — error-handling#2 — Fix: D1 revised, menu paths call the gate directly; registration failure notified.
* **I13** `destroy()` rejection has no surface or fallback — error-handling#3 — Fix: notify and fall back to `exit(0)` inside the gate.
* **I14** scan-throw cause dropped — error-handling#4 — Fix: logged with tab id via `warnSafely`.
* **I15** real-app dirty-close assertion was optional — tests#1 — Fix: mandatory, uses the harness's existing tree edit.
* **I16** File → Exit revert not caught by any proof — tests#2 — Fix: quit-gate spec covers compact File → Exit; async-errors asserts destroy.
* **I17** O4's "allow-close already permits in effect" is false — ipc-contract#6 (nit) — Fix: wording corrected.
* **I18** scan via `readTree` migrates (writes) legacy trees — persisted-state#2 — Fix: read-only inspection, no migration write, no status change.
* **I19** freshness `conflict`/`unavailable` tab with `dirty === false` holds the only copy — persisted-state#3 — Fix: O2 blocks on those states.
* **I20** tests do not prove writer/reader agreement — persisted-state#4 — Fix: O5 unit assertions (same store object, unchanged bytes and freshness, tree-key read).

Each CloseRequested is a new `CloseRequestedEvent` with `_preventDefault = false`. Concrete input: one dirty analysis tab; user clicks title-bar × (or File → Exit); the first handler preventDefaults and opens the summary dialog; user clicks × / File → Exit / Alt+F4 again. A handler that returns early because the dialog is already open, without calling `preventDefault()` on the second event, lets the wrapper `destroy()` the window and the dirty tree is discarded. The same destroy happens if the first handler is still awaiting the scan and has not preventDefaulted yet. ### lens-chess-semantics-r1.txt

The plan’s obligations are a quit gate, a dirty/uninspectable-tab predicate, a summary dialog, a capability, and verification. None of those compare positions, reuse move paths, derive parity, pick variations, or rebuild trees.

NOT APPLICABLE: this plan never mutates or compares the in-memory game tree, a FEN, or a move path. O2 only reads the existing `dirty` flag and tree-storage status; O1/O3/O4/O5 are window close, dialog, capability, and tests. Chess-tree invariants (position identity, path validity, root-FEN parity, variation vs mainline, legality, round-trip of headers/comments) cannot be introduced or broken by that contract.

VERDICT: APPROVED

### lens-chess-semantics-r6.txt

NOT APPLICABLE: this quit-gate plan (r6 O2/D2 included) only reads `dirty`, file freshness, and tree-storage inspectability; it does not compare FENs, mutate or reuse move paths, derive ply or colour, pick a variation child, decide legality, or rebuild a tree, headers, or comments. Drift `GameNotation.tsx` halfmove numbering is outside these obligations.

VERDICT: APPROVED

### lens-correctness-r1.txt

The close-request gate’s `preventDefault` contract is inconsistent with Tauri’s JS wrapper: a second close while the dialog is open (or while the scan is in flight) can still `destroy()` the window and drop dirty trees.

```
[blocker] /tmp/drain-plan-drain-90a2aa88-slot0/build/plan-r1.body.md:134 — A second close request while the quit dialog is open (or while the dirty scan is still awaiting) is specified only as “do not open a second dialog.” `@tauri-apps/api` `Window.onCloseRequested` (`window.js:1632-1639`) awaits the handler and then `destroy()`s unless *that* event’s `preventDefault()` ran. Each CloseRequested is a new `CloseRequestedEvent` with `_preventDefault = false`. Concrete input: one dirty analysis tab; user clicks title-bar × (or File → Exit); the first handler preventDefaults and opens the summary dialog; user clicks × / File → Exit / Alt+F4 again. A handler that returns early because the dialog is already open, without calling `preventDefault()` on the second event, lets the wrapper `destroy()` the window and the dirty tree is discarded. The same destroy happens if the first handler is still awaiting the scan and has not preventDefaulted yet. The allow-quit path is the only one that may omit preventDefault (or must `destroy()` itself). (confidence: 94)

[should-fix] /tmp/drain-plan-drain-90a2aa88-slot0/build/plan-r1.body.md:117 — O2 says a store created only to inspect an unmounted tab “is disposed” and must not write storage or leave a store behind. `TreeStore.dispose` is a no-op (`src/state/store/tree.ts:821-822`); `closeTreeStore` (`tree.ts:237-243`) is tab-close teardown and also `removeFileFreshness`s a still-open tab. Following the plan as written either leaves a persist-backed store (and can migrate/write on hydrate) or tears down live tree/freshness before Cancel. The scan must treat `getCachedTreeStore` as authoritative for a still-open tab and must not call `closeTreeStore` on it; uncached tabs should be read through `tabStorage.readTree` (pending writes included), which is the path `closeTab` already uses via `createTreeStore`. (confidence: 88)

[should-fix] /tmp/drain-plan-drain-90a2aa88-slot0/build/plan-r1.body.md:132 — Cancel “activates the first listed tab.” O3’s fail-closed arm opens the same dialog with an empty list. Cancel then has no tab to activate; `activeTabAtom` (`src/state/atoms.ts:181-183`) turns a non-member id into `null` and unmounts the current board. Cancel on a scan throw must keep the current tab. (confidence: 85)

[should-fix] /tmp/drain-plan-drain-90a2aa88-slot0/build/plan-r1.body.md:160 — O5’s Playwright spec clicks the title-bar close and expects the gate, but the close→CloseRequested hook is optional. Production `close()` is `plugin:window|close` and then `tauri://close-requested`; e2e `fixtures.ts` has neither `plugin:window|close` nor `plugin:window|destroy`, and `close` does not emit `tauri://close-requested`. Without that producer/consumer pair, the click either throws on unexpected IPC or never runs the listener, so the spec does not prove the production close path. (confidence: 82)
```

Limitation: `.claude/skills/verify-ui/SKILL.md` was not readable (permission deny); verification judgment used O5 and `docs/localization.md` only.

VERDICT: REVISE

### lens-correctness-r2.txt

I1 CLOSED — O1 requires every `onCloseRequested` delivery to call `preventDefault()` synchronously before any await; Tauri then skips the wrapper `destroy()` (`window.js:1632-1640`). The gate destroys only when quitting is allowed, so a second close, a scan error, and a gate exception keep the window.

I9 CLOSED — O2 reads a cached store via `getCachedTreeStore(tab.value)` (`tree.ts:147`), forbids `closeTreeStore`/`createTreeStore`, and reads uncached tabs at `getTabTreeKey(tab)` through a non-migrating inspection (`tabStorage.ts:496-501`).

I10 CLOSED — O3 fail-closed Cancel keeps the current route and tab; listed-tab Cancel still navigates to `/` and activates the first listed tab.

I11 CLOSED — O5 requires `plugin:window|close` to emit `tauri://close-requested` to the registered window listener and `plugin:window|destroy` to be recorded with no further emit, matching `window.js:944-962` and `event.js:19`.

[blocker] tasks/plans/.plan-2026-11-01-quit-gate-unsaved-tabs.md-r2.body.md:217 — O5’s mandatory dirty-tab `verify:app` input is the file-backed PGN edit at `scripts/verify-app.mjs:3249-3321` (GO12 second file game, origin `kind: "file"`). `autoSaveAtom` defaults to true (`src/state/atoms.ts:507-511`) and `BoardAnalysis.tsx:112-115` auto-saves that origin whenever `dirty` is true, so title-bar close can observe `dirty === false` and destroy immediately with no dialog. The harness already seeds `localStorage.setItem("auto-save", "false")` at `verify-app.mjs:3691-3697`; O5 does not require that seed or a tab auto-save does not cover (origin `none`, `databaseDraft`, conflict/unavailable). (confidence: 92)

[should-fix] tasks/plans/.plan-2026-11-01-quit-gate-unsaved-tabs.md-r2.body.md:140 — O2’s cached-store procedure never consults `tabStorage.getStatus(getTabTreeKey(tab))`. A cached analysis/play tab with `dirty === false` and status `unreadable`/`unavailable` (default store after persist mapped the gated read to `null`, `tabStorage.ts:443-445`) is allowed to quit, while `BoardsPage.tsx:124-132` refuses to close that same tab. The predicate’s uninspectable arm is not applied on the cached path. (confidence: 86)

[should-fix] tasks/plans/.plan-2026-11-01-quit-gate-unsaved-tabs.md-r2.body.md:144 — Uncached inspection does not pin `requireExisting` to the production persist flag `treeKey !== tab.value` (`tree.ts:799`). A never-mounted analysis tab whose `treeKey` is missing is `unavailable` under that flag and `absent` if the new method defaults `requireExisting` to false (`tabStorage.ts:476-484`), so the uninspectable arm does not fire. ### lens-ipc-contract-r1.txt

The close-request protocol is the IPC surface this plan adds. Tauri’s JS `onCloseRequested` default is `destroy()` unless that delivery calls `preventDefault()`, and Rust already `prevent_close()`s as soon as a JS listener exists. The plan never writes that keep-alive signal on every path that must stay open.

[blocker] `@tauri-apps/api` `window.js:1632-1639` and plan O3 — `onCloseRequested` awaits the handler, then calls `this.destroy()` unless **that** `CloseRequestedEvent` had `preventDefault()`. Rust (`tauri-2.10.2` `window.rs:167-169`) has already `prevent_close()`d because a JS listener exists. A second title-bar × / File → Exit / Cmd+Q while the summary dialog is open is a second delivery of `tauri://close-requested`. O3 only says that delivery “keeps the one dialog”; a handler that returns because the dialog is already open leaves `_preventDefault` false, so `plugin:window|destroy` runs and the dirty trees are discarded. Every keep-alive path (dirty scan, dialog already open, scan throw) has to call `preventDefault()` on that event, and it has to do so before the handler returns to Tauri. (confidence: 93)

[blocker] `src/platform/useTauriListener.ts:38-47` and plan O1 — O1’s “one listener, unlisten on unmount including before the listen promise settles” is the contract of `useTauriListener`. That helper invokes the callback with `void (async () => { await callback(...) })()` and returns at once. `onCloseRequested` then sees no `preventDefault()` and runs `destroy()` **before** the scan or dialog can keep the window. The function passed to `onCloseRequested` has to be the function Tauri awaits (same shape as `watchMaximized`’s `onResized` wiring in `src/routes/-appMenu.ts:498-512`). `useTauriListener` cannot sit between them. ### lens-persisted-state-r1.txt

The plan is in this lens: it scans persisted tab trees on quit and must keep writer, reader, keys, and tab-store lifetime aligned.

[blocker] `src/state/store/tree.ts:147,237,300,821` / plan O2 — the quit scan’s reader is unspecified, and the traced `closeTab` path is the wrong one. `keepMounted={false}` only unmounts the React view; a visited tab’s store stays in `treeStores` (`dispose` is a no-op). `closeTab` then does `createTreeStore(value, undefined, getTabTreeKey(tab))`. Copying that for quit, then “disposing” with `closeTreeStore` to satisfy “must not leave a store behind,” evicts a workspace-owned cache and runs `removeFileFreshness`. Concrete case: dirty tab A, switch to B, File → Exit, Cancel. A’s live store is gone; remount hydrates from the last pending/storage frame and can split from the still-mounted view if the active tab was evicted too. After a webview reload, a background tab with `treeKey !== value` and `createTreeStore(tab.value)` hydrates and can seed the logical id, while the dirty tree lives at `getTabTreeKey` (`src/state/workspaceTypes.ts:31`). Writer (persist name = physical key, pending map) and reader (logical id / new default store) disagree; the user loses or silently replaces that tab’s edits. (confidence: 93)

[blocker] `src/state/store/tabStorage.ts:453-504` / plan O2 — a scan that hydrates through `readTree` / `createTreeStore` is a writer. `readTree` rewrites legacy uncompressed trees (`setItem` at `:496-501`); persist `getItem` uses that path. Concrete case: several large imported games after a reload, then title-bar close. The gate hydrates every unmounted tab at once, migrates them into the shared ~5 MB sessionStorage quota, and `setItem` can fail or evict other keys during the confirm. O2 says “must not write” but names no read-only API (`readForClone` is migrate-false; `read()` collapses unreadable/unavailable/absent to `null` and would treat an unreadable tree as clean). Phase 1 files do not add an inspect helper on `tabStorage`. (confidence: 90)

[should-fix] `src/components/tabs/FileFreshnessGate.tsx:250-261` / plan O2 — a conflict or unavailable tab can hold the only copy of a game with `dirty === false` (`sourceStamp === null` and disk PGN differs; missing game). The mandate names that panel’s “Save my version as a new game” tree. O2 only blocks on `dirty` or an unreadable/unavailable *storage* status, so quit still drops that tree when sessionStorage is cleared. (confidence: 84)

[should-fix] plan O5 — unit tests as listed will not prove writer/reader agreement: they do not require `getCachedTreeStore(id)` to be the same object after the scan, `sessionStorage` bytes unchanged, or inspection of a tab with `treeKey !== value`. A `createTreeStore` + `closeTreeStore` implementation can pass the stated matrix. (confidence: 88)

VERDICT: REVISE

### lens-platform-semantics-r1.txt

The quit-gate mechanism is the same `CloseRequested` path on Linux (GTK `delete-event`), Windows (`WM_CLOSE` / `WindowMessage::Close`), and macOS (`windowShouldClose`). File → Exit becomes `close()` instead of `exit(0)`, which otherwise skips that path (`RequestExit` → `ExitRequested { code: Some }`). macOS predefined Quit is `terminate:` and is replaced by a custom item with `cmd+q`. That matches muda/tao. `allow-destroy` is required on every OS once a JS `onCloseRequested` listener exists, because the API then `prevent_close`s and the JS default calls `destroy()`.

No Rust/`cfg` change, so `rust-windows-test`, `rust-macos-test`, `rust-platform`, `pnpm rust:windows:check`, and the FreeBSD `target_os` probe do not apply. Runtime proof of the gate stays Linux `verify:app` plus JS tests that cover both `isMacOS` trees. Dock Quit / OS logout remain the documented native terminate hole.

VERDICT: APPROVED

Close/quit delivery (O1), macOS Quit replacement, `destroy`/`allow-destroy`, and O5’s menu/listener/`verify:app` proofs are unchanged. No Linux/macOS/Windows path, handle, `cfg`, or ABI premise in O1/O4/O5 is invalidated.

### lens-tauri-security-r1.txt

It does not acquire, persist, migrate, or return bearer tokens; it does not change keyring or session-credential records; it does not join, resolve, or mutate filesystem paths; it does not install or verify signed artifacts; and it states no Rust change to `ExitGuard` or command diagnostics. `sessionStorage` here is chess tab trees, not credential material. Capability `core:window:allow-destroy` and listener lifetime are owned by `review-ipc-contract`.

### plan-r1.md

# Plan: one quit gate for unsaved tabs (f-20261101-13)

## Goal

Quitting ChessFable — title-bar close, native window close (decoration ×, Alt+F4, window-manager
close), File → Exit, and macOS Quit / Cmd+Q — never discards a tab with unsaved tree edits without
the user first confirming. A quit with nothing unsaved stays immediate.

## MANDATE

Verbatim from `tasks/findings.md`, f-20261101-13 (frontend-state, entry build, impact data-loss):

> **Where:** `src/routes/__root.tsx` (menu Exit is `exit(0)`), `src/components/TopBar.tsx` (title-bar close calls `windowControls.close()`), `src-tauri/src/main.rs` `app.run` (handles only `WindowEvent::Destroyed` and `ExitRequested`, `ExitGuard` sequences teardown only); the tab list (`src/state/atoms.ts:106,144`) and every tree (`src/state/store/tabStorage.ts:470-569`) live in `sessionStorage`; the only dirty check is `closeTab` in `src/components/tabs/BoardsPage.tsx:133`.
>
> **Defect:** no code listens for a close request (no `onCloseRequested` / `CloseRequested` anywhere in `src` or `src-tauri/src`). The ledger measured on 2026-09-22 (around line 12095) that this `sessionStorage` is cleared when the window closes, so the `beforeunload`/`pagehide` flush does not carry dirty trees across a quit.
>
> **Failure scenario:** with auto-save off, or with a tab auto-save does not cover (a file tab parked in the conflict/unavailable panel, a database draft, an origin-`none` board from paste, URL or FEN import), the user annotates and then closes the window or picks File → Exit: the app quits at once and the edits are gone. The conflict panel's "Save my version as a new game" tree is lost too.
>
> **Open question:** what does a single quit gate look like across window close, menu Exit and Cmd+Q (intercept `onCloseRequested` plus the menu path), which tab states count as needing a decision, and does it reuse `ConfirmChangesModal` per tab or one summary dialog? First confirm with a real-app probe (`pnpm verify:app`) that tab trees do not survive a restart.
>
> **Related:** f-20260831-16 rejected a `beforeunload` confirm only as the answer to flush-failure reporting, not for dirty tabs on quit.

## Threat model and non-goals

No adversary. The inputs are the user's own quit gestures and the app's own persisted tab state.
Environments: Linux (WebKitGTK, custom title bar or native GTK decorations), Windows (WebView2),
macOS (WKWebView, native app menu). A renderer that is hung or crashed cannot run any gate; that
is a known limit, not a target. A process kill (SIGKILL, power loss, OS logout that terminates the
process) is out of scope.

## Traced premises

Frozen at BASE `095048137821e5d7a03c6ae797f7e12153c0fa71`.

* `src/routes/__root.tsx:203` — menu callback `exit: () => exit(0)`; `exit` comes from
  `@/platform/native` (`src/platform/native.ts:16`, re-export of `@tauri-apps/plugin-process`).
  It is the only production `exit(` call in `src`.
* `tauri-plugin-process-2.3.1/src/commands.rs:8-9` — `exit` is `app.exit(code)`;
  `tauri-runtime-wry-2.10.0/src/lib.rs:4215-4227` — that becomes `RunEvent::ExitRequested
  { code: Some(code) }`, never a window `CloseRequested`. So a window close-request listener does
  not see File → Exit today.
* `src/components/TopBar.tsx:170-173,278` and `src/routes/-appMenu.ts:285-294` — the title-bar ×
  calls `appWindow.close()`. `tauri-runtime-wry-2.10.0/src/lib.rs:4168-4169` (native
  `TaoWindowEvent::CloseRequested`) and `:4231-4232` (`WindowMessage::Close`) both route to
  `on_close_requested` (`:4265-4290`), which closes unless a handler signals prevent.
* `tauri-2.10.2/src/manager/window.rs:167-170` — when the window has a JS listener for
  `tauri://close-requested`, Tauri prevents the close and emits the event to the window.
  `@tauri-apps/api@2.11.1` `window.js:1632-1640` — `onCloseRequested(handler)` awaits the
  handler and calls `this.destroy()` unless the handler called `preventDefault()`.
* `src-tauri/capabilities/main.json:22` grants `core:window:allow-close`; there is no
  `core:window:allow-destroy`, which `destroy()` needs.
* `src-tauri/src/main.rs:2457-2490` — `RunEvent::WindowEvent::Destroyed` cancels the window's
  operations; `ExitRequested` (both `code: None` after the last window is destroyed,
  `tauri-runtime-wry-2.10.0/src/lib.rs:4171-4185`, and `code: Some`) runs `ExitGuard` cleanup and
  then exits. Destroying the only window therefore reaches the same backend teardown as
  `exit(0)`.
* `src/routes/-appMenu.ts:87-97` — on macOS the application menu's Quit is the predefined
  `item: "Quit"` (muda `PredefinedMenuItemType::Quit`), whose native terminate path
  (`tao-0.34.5/src/platform_impl/macos/app_delegate.rs:63,133`, `applicationWillTerminate`)
  emits neither a window close request nor a renderer callback. `-appMenu.ts:132-140` — File →
  Exit is offered only off macOS.
* `src/components/tabs/BoardsPage.tsx:117-135` — `closeTab` asks only when
  `isPersistentGameOrigin(closedTab) && store.getState().dirty`; `src/utils/tabs.ts:65-68`
  `isPersistentGameOrigin` is false for origin `none`. `BoardsPage.tsx:302` renders tab panels
  with `keepMounted={false}`, so an inactive tab has no mounted tree store; `closeTab` reads one
  through `createTreeStore(value, undefined, getTabTreeKey(closedTab))` and disposes it.
* `src/state/store/tree.ts` — `dirty` is part of the persisted tree state, set by every editing
  action (`:388`, `:640-888`); `src/components/boards/BoardAnalysis.tsx:112-115` auto-saves only
  persistent, non-`databaseDraft` origins and only when `autoSaveAtom` is on.
* `src/state/store/tabStorage.ts:507-509` — per-tab tree status `not-read | available |
  unreadable | unavailable`; `:945-946` the `beforeunload`/`pagehide` best-effort flush
  (`d-20260901-14`: never throws, never notifies).
* `src/components/tabs/ConfirmChangesModal.tsx` — a single-tab dialog bound to tab close
  (`onSaved` closes the tab; `Tab.UnsavedChanges`, `Tab.UnsavedChangesConfirm` "…before
  closing?", `Tab.CloseWithoutSaving`).
* `scripts/verify-app.mjs:326-340,3976-3984` — `pnpm verify:app` exits the real app by clicking
  the title-bar close control and asserts the process exits; it must keep passing with no dirty
  tab.
* `docs/localization.md:3` — `pnpm i18n:check` requires every new key non-empty in all 16
  shipped catalogues under `src/translation/`.
* Session-storage premise: `tasks/findings.md` f-20260922-10 records "measured 2026-09-22 — the
  backing store is `sessionStorage` … which is cleared when the window closes". The MANDATE asks
  for a fresh real-app probe; planner mode cannot launch the app, so the probe is a Phase 2 proof
  obligation (see Risks).

## Approach

### O1 — One interception point for every quit gesture

The window's close request is the single gate. The renderer registers exactly one
`onCloseRequested` listener on the current window (obtained through `@/platform/native`, as
`TopBar` already does for `onResized`, `d-20260831-30`) for the lifetime of the root route, with
the matching unlisten on unmount, including an unmount that happens before the listen promise
settles.

* Title-bar × and native window close already raise the close request (Traced premises).
* File → Exit stops calling `exit(0)` and instead requests the window close, so it passes through
  the same gate; after the gate allows it, the window is destroyed, and the existing
  last-window `ExitRequested` path in `main.rs` runs the unchanged backend teardown. The `exit`
  re-export becomes unused in `__root.tsx`.
* On macOS the application menu's predefined `Quit` item is replaced by an app-defined item with
  the same label and the `cmd+q` shortcut, whose action is the same window close request. This is
  what brings Cmd+Q under the gate; the predefined item cannot be intercepted (Traced premises).
* No Rust change: `ExitGuard` and the `Destroyed` handling stay as they are.

### O2 — Which tabs need a decision

A shared predicate decides, per tab, whether quitting would lose work the user has not saved:

* tab type `analysis` or `play`, and
* its tree state is `dirty`, whatever its `gameOrigin` kind (file, database, database draft,
  `none`) — auto-save on or off, freshness conflict/unavailable or not; or
* its tree cannot be inspected (tree-storage status `unreadable` or `unavailable`, or reading it
  fails), because "could not check" must not be treated as "saved".

`puzzles` and `new` tabs never block (their tree is not user-authored; see Not part of this task).
The scan covers every tab in the workspace, active or not, mounted or not. Reading an unmounted
tab's tree must not write storage, change its read status destructively, or leave a store behind
(any store created for the read is disposed). The predicate lives in one exported helper so the
tab-close prompt can route through it too; that rerouting is f-20261101-14's change, not this
plan's (see Not part of this task), so there is one predicate, not a second copy.

### O3 — The quit dialog

When the scan finds blocking tabs, the close request is prevented and one summary dialog opens
(not `ConfirmChangesModal` per tab — see Decisions):

* Title: the existing `Tab.UnsavedChanges` string. Body: a new message saying the listed tabs have
  unsaved changes that are lost on quit, followed by the tab names; an uninspectable tab is listed
  with a short "could not be checked" marker.
* **Cancel** (default focus, Escape and outside click map to it): closes the dialog, keeps the app
  running and activates the first listed tab, so the user lands where saving is needed.
* **Quit without saving**: destroys the window directly (it must not re-enter the gate).
* While the dialog is open, a repeated close request does not open a second dialog or stack
  listeners; it keeps the one dialog.
* If the scan itself throws, the gate fails closed: the dialog opens with the generic message and
  no list, and still offers both buttons, so the app can always be quit.
* When the scan finds nothing, the close proceeds immediately with no dialog (the existing
  `verify:app` close assertion depends on this).

New user-facing strings go through i18next, with non-empty values in all 16 catalogues
(`pnpm i18n:extract`, `pnpm i18n:check`).

### O4 — Capability

`src-tauri/capabilities/main.json` gains exactly `core:window:allow-destroy` for the main window,
needed by both the default `onCloseRequested` path and Quit without saving. Justification
(ipc-events rule): it grants destroying the app's own window, which `allow-close` already permits
in effect; nothing else widens.

### O5 — Verification

* Unit tests: the predicate over every tab type × origin kind × dirty × tree-status case,
  including an unmounted tab read and a read that throws; the gate's allow / prevent / fail-closed
  decisions; the listener's single registration and unlisten (including unmount-before-settle).
* Menu tests (`src/routes/-appMenu.test.ts`): File → Exit and the macOS app-menu Quit both invoke
  the close request, the macOS Quit carries `cmd+q`, and no predefined `Quit` item remains.
* Component test: the dialog lists names, Cancel keeps the app and activates the first tab, Quit
  without saving calls destroy exactly once, a second close request while open opens nothing new.
* Mocked-IPC Playwright (`pnpm test:e2e:container`): a spec that dirties a tab, clicks the
  title-bar close, asserts the dialog (committed snapshot) and that destroy is not called, then
  Quit without saving asserts destroy is called; a clean close asserts destroy with no dialog.
* Real app (`pnpm verify:app`): the existing clean close still exits; a dirty-tab close keeps the
  process alive with the dialog visible, and Quit without saving then exits with no child left; a
  restart under the same throwaway `HOME` shows the dirty tab is not restored (the MANDATE's
  probe). If the harness cannot produce a dirty tab without native chrome, the phase reports that
  limit explicitly and the e2e spec carries the dialog proof.

## Decisions and trade-offs

Decided autonomously (full auto):

* **D1 — Interception point:** the window close request is the single gate; File → Exit and
  macOS Quit route into it. Rejected: a renderer `requestQuit()` called from each entry point in
  addition to `onCloseRequested` (two gates that must agree; native close and Alt+F4 still need
  the listener anyway). Rejected: a Rust-side `CloseRequested`/`ExitRequested` prevent that asks
  the renderer over a new event (new IPC event and round trip for a decision only the renderer can
  make). Reversal: route the menu actions back to `exit(0)` and keep the listener.
* **D2 — Tab states that need a decision:** dirty `analysis`/`play` trees of every origin, plus
  uninspectable trees; puzzles and new tabs never block. Rejected: only persistent origins (the
  `closeTab` predicate) — the MANDATE names origin-`none` boards and database drafts. Rejected:
  ignoring uninspectable trees — "could not check" is not "saved" under a data-loss impact.
  Reversal: narrow the shared predicate.
* **D3 — Summary dialog, not per-tab `ConfirmChangesModal`:** one dialog with Cancel / Quit
  without saving. Rejected: chaining `ConfirmChangesModal` per dirty tab — it is bound to closing
  one tab (its `onSaved` closes the tab, its copy says "before closing"), n tabs means n dialogs,
  and origin-`none`/draft saves need their own picker flows mid-quit. Saving stays on the normal
  per-tab save surfaces after Cancel. Precedent: the existing unsaved-changes prompt on tab close
  (`BoardsPage.tsx:133`, `Tab.UnsavedChanges`) — the same promise extended to quitting, so this is
  not a novel product question. Reversal: add a "Save all" action later without changing the gate.
* **D4 — Fail closed with a way out:** a scan error still shows the dialog with both buttons.
  Rejected: fail open (silent data loss on a bug); rejected: block quit entirely (an app that
  cannot be closed).

## Risks / open questions

* **Session-storage premise not yet re-measured.** Planner mode cannot run `pnpm verify:app`.
  The 2026-09-22 measurement and WebKitGTK's in-memory session storage both say trees do not
  survive a restart; Phase 2's restart assertion re-measures it. If it shows trees *do* survive,
  the gate is still correct for file/database tabs whose saved copy is older, but the dialog
  copy ("lost on quit") would be wrong — stop and return to planning rather than ship that copy.
* macOS and Windows cannot be run on this machine. The macOS Quit routing is proven by the menu
  unit test and the Traced premises; native confirmation on macOS/Windows is not available here
  and is reported as not observed.
* Dock-menu Quit and OS logout on macOS go through the native terminate path and stay outside the
  gate (known limit; tao 0.34.5 exposes no `applicationShouldTerminate` hook).

## Not part of this task

* Rerouting `closeTab` through the shared predicate (origin-`none` dirty tab closes without a
  prompt): f-20261101-14, a co-located finding worked by the adopting session after the phases.
* f-20261101-18 (Elo `?` parsing).
* Persisting tabs across restarts, a "Save all" action, or saving from inside the quit dialog.
* Puzzle session lists that live in `sessionStorage` (not user-authored tree edits).
* Any change to `ExitGuard`, backend teardown, or the `beforeunload`/`pagehide` flush.

## Phases

### Phase 1 — Gate, predicate, dialog, menu routing, capability

* Obligations: O1, O2, O3, O4, and O5's unit, menu and component tests.
* Files: `src/routes/__root.tsx`, `src/routes/-appMenu.ts`, `src/routes/-appMenu.test.ts`,
  `src/utils/tabs.ts` (or a new sibling module for the predicate/gate), a new quit-dialog
  component under `src/components/` with its test, `src-tauri/capabilities/main.json`,
  `src/translation/*.json` (16 catalogues).
* Sensitive: persistence reads (`src/state/**` read paths), listener lifetime, a capability
  change. Role: `sensitive`.
* PROOF COMMAND (the vitest paths must include every new test file the phase adds): `pnpm vitest run src/routes src/utils src/components/tabs src/components/TopBar.window.test.ts && pnpm i18n:extract && pnpm i18n:check && pnpm lint`

### Phase 2 — Real-app and e2e proof
