  mock needs a close-requested/destroy hook), a `quit-gate` project in `playwright.config.ts`,
  `scripts/verify-app.mjs`.
* Role: `normal`.
* PROOF COMMAND: `pnpm test:e2e:container --project=quit-gate && pnpm build && pnpm verify:app`

### plan-r2.md

# Plan: one quit gate for unsaved tabs (f-20261101-13)

## Goal

Quitting ChessFable — title-bar close, native window close (decoration ×, Alt+F4, window-manager
close), File → Exit, and macOS Quit / Cmd+Q — never discards a tab with unsaved tree edits without
the user first confirming. A quit with nothing unsaved stays immediate.

## MANDATE

Verbatim from `tasks/findings.md`, f-20261101-13 (frontend-state, entry build, impact data-loss):

> **Where:** `src/routes/__root.tsx` (menu Exit is `exit(0)`), `src/components/TopBar.tsx` (title-bar close calls `windowControls.close()`), `src-tauri/src/main.rs` `app.run` (handles only `WindowEvent::Destroyed` and `ExitRequested`, `ExitGuard` sequences teardown only); the tab list (`src/state/atoms.ts:106,144`) and every tree (`src/state/store/tabStorage.ts:470-569`) live in `sessionStorage`; the only dirty check is `closeTab` in `src/components/tabs/BoardsPage.tsx:133`.
>
> **Defect:** no code listens for a close request (no `onCloseRequested` / `CloseRequested` anywhere in `src` or `src-tauri/src`). The ledger measured on 2026-09-22 (around line 12095) that this `sessionStorage` is cleared when the window closes, so the `beforeunload`/`pagehide` flush does not carry dirty trees across a quit.
>
> **Failure scenario:** with auto-save off, or with a tab auto-save does not cover (a file tab parked in the conflict/unavailable panel, a database draft, an origin-`none` board from paste, URL or FEN import), the user annotates and then closes the window or picks File → Exit: the app quits at once and the edits are gone. The conflict panel's "Save my version as a new game" tree is lost too.
>
> **Open question:** what does a single quit gate look like across window close, menu Exit and Cmd+Q (intercept `onCloseRequested` plus the menu path), which tab states count as needing a decision, and does it reuse `ConfirmChangesModal` per tab or one summary dialog? First confirm with a real-app probe (`pnpm verify:app`) that tab trees do not survive a restart.
>
> **Related:** f-20260831-16 rejected a `beforeunload` confirm only as the answer to flush-failure reporting, not for dirty tabs on quit.

## Threat model and non-goals

No adversary. The inputs are the user's own quit gestures and the app's own persisted tab state.
Environments: Linux (WebKitGTK, custom title bar or native GTK decorations), Windows (WebView2),
macOS (WKWebView, native app menu). A renderer that is hung or crashed cannot run any gate; that
is a known limit, not a target. A process kill (SIGKILL, power loss, OS logout that terminates the
process) is out of scope.

## Traced premises

Frozen at BASE `095048137821e5d7a03c6ae797f7e12153c0fa71`.

* `src/routes/__root.tsx:203` — menu callback `exit: () => exit(0)`; `exit` comes from
  `@/platform/native` (`src/platform/native.ts:16`, re-export of `@tauri-apps/plugin-process`).
  It is the only production `exit(` call in `src`.
* `tauri-plugin-process-2.3.1/src/commands.rs:8-9` — `exit` is `app.exit(code)`;
  `tauri-runtime-wry-2.10.0/src/lib.rs:4215-4227` — that becomes `RunEvent::ExitRequested
  { code: Some(code) }`, never a window `CloseRequested`. So a window close-request listener does
  not see File → Exit today.
* `src/components/TopBar.tsx:170-173,278` and `src/routes/-appMenu.ts:285-294` — the title-bar ×
  calls `appWindow.close()`. `tauri-runtime-wry-2.10.0/src/lib.rs:4168-4169` (native
  `TaoWindowEvent::CloseRequested`) and `:4231-4232` (`WindowMessage::Close`) both route to
  `on_close_requested` (`:4265-4290`), which closes unless a handler signals prevent.
* `tauri-2.10.2/src/manager/window.rs:167-170` — when the window has a JS listener for
  `tauri://close-requested`, Tauri prevents the close and emits the event to the window.
  `@tauri-apps/api@2.11.1` `window.js:1632-1640` — `onCloseRequested(handler)` awaits the
  handler and calls `this.destroy()` unless the handler called `preventDefault()`.
* `src-tauri/capabilities/main.json:22` grants `core:window:allow-close`; there is no
  `core:window:allow-destroy`, which `destroy()` needs.
* `src-tauri/src/main.rs:2457-2490` — `RunEvent::WindowEvent::Destroyed` cancels the window's
  operations; `ExitRequested` (both `code: None` after the last window is destroyed,
  `tauri-runtime-wry-2.10.0/src/lib.rs:4171-4185`, and `code: Some`) runs `ExitGuard` cleanup and
  then exits. Destroying the only window therefore reaches the same backend teardown as
  `exit(0)`.
* `src/routes/-appMenu.ts:87-97` — on macOS the application menu's Quit is the predefined
  `item: "Quit"` (muda `PredefinedMenuItemType::Quit`), whose native terminate path
  (`tao-0.34.5/src/platform_impl/macos/app_delegate.rs:63,133`, `applicationWillTerminate`)
  emits neither a window close request nor a renderer callback. `-appMenu.ts:132-140` — File →
  Exit is offered only off macOS.
* `src/components/tabs/BoardsPage.tsx:117-135` — `closeTab` asks only when
  `isPersistentGameOrigin(closedTab) && store.getState().dirty`; `src/utils/tabs.ts:65-68`
  `isPersistentGameOrigin` is false for origin `none`. `BoardsPage.tsx:302` renders tab panels
  with `keepMounted={false}`, so an inactive tab has no mounted tree store; `closeTab` reads one
  through `createTreeStore(value, undefined, getTabTreeKey(closedTab))` and disposes it.
* `src/state/store/tree.ts` — `dirty` is part of the persisted tree state, set by every editing
  action (`:388`, `:640-888`); `src/components/boards/BoardAnalysis.tsx:112-115` auto-saves only
  persistent, non-`databaseDraft` origins and only when `autoSaveAtom` is on.
* `src/state/store/tabStorage.ts:507-509` — per-tab tree status `not-read | available |
  unreadable | unavailable`; `:945-946` the `beforeunload`/`pagehide` best-effort flush
  (`d-20260901-14`: never throws, never notifies).
* `src/components/tabs/ConfirmChangesModal.tsx` — a single-tab dialog bound to tab close
  (`onSaved` closes the tab; `Tab.UnsavedChanges`, `Tab.UnsavedChangesConfirm` "…before
  closing?", `Tab.CloseWithoutSaving`).
* `scripts/verify-app.mjs:326-340,3976-3984` — `pnpm verify:app` exits the real app by clicking
  the title-bar close control and asserts the process exits; it must keep passing with no dirty
  tab.
* `docs/localization.md:3` — `pnpm i18n:check` requires every new key non-empty in all 16
  shipped catalogues under `src/translation/`.
* Session-storage premise: `tasks/findings.md` f-20260922-10 records "measured 2026-09-22 — the
  backing store is `sessionStorage` … which is cleared when the window closes". The MANDATE asks
  for a fresh real-app probe; planner mode cannot launch the app, so the probe is a Phase 2 proof
  obligation (see Risks).

## Approach

### O1 — One quit gate, reached from every quit gesture

One renderer function, the quit gate, decides every quit. It has two entry points and no other:

* **Close-request listener.** The root route registers exactly one `onCloseRequested` listener on
  the current window (obtained through `@/platform/native`, as `TopBar` does for `onResized`,
  `d-20260831-30`), with the matching unlisten on unmount, including an unmount that happens before
  the listen promise settles. The function handed to `onCloseRequested` is the function Tauri
  awaits; it is not wrapped in `useTauriListener`, whose fire-and-forget callback
  (`src/platform/useTauriListener.ts:37-45`) would let the wrapper destroy the window before the
  gate runs. **Every delivery calls `preventDefault()` on its own event synchronously, before any
  await**, and then runs the gate; the gate itself destroys the window when quitting is allowed.
  Consequently a second close request while the dialog is open or the scan is running, a scan
  error, and an exception anywhere in the gate all keep the window. This covers the title-bar ×,
  the native decoration ×, Alt+F4 and window-manager close (Traced premises).
* **Menu actions.** File → Exit stops calling `exit(0)` and calls the quit gate directly. On macOS
  the application menu's predefined `Quit` item is replaced by an app-defined item with the same
  label and the `cmd+q` shortcut whose action is the same direct call; the predefined item cannot
  be intercepted (Traced premises). Because the menu paths call the gate directly, they stay gated
  even if the close-request listener failed to register. The `exit` import leaves `__root.tsx`.

When quitting is allowed the gate destroys the window; the existing last-window `ExitRequested`
path in `main.rs` runs the unchanged backend teardown. No Rust change.

Failure contract:

* Listener registration rejects: notified once through the same `notifyUnlessCancelled` route
  `watchMaximized` uses (`src/routes/-appMenu.ts:498-512`), and logged. Native window close is then
  ungated for that session (known limit; the menu paths remain gated).
* `destroy()` rejects (clean quit or Quit without saving): the error is notified, and the gate falls
  back to `exit(0)` through `@/platform/native`, which reaches the same backend teardown, so the app
  can always be quit. The fallback lives inside the gate, not at the menu call sites.
* While one gate run is in progress or its dialog is open, a further entry (either entry point)
  does not start a second scan or open a second dialog.

### O2 — Which tabs need a decision

A tab blocks the quit when quitting would lose work that exists nowhere else:

* tab type `analysis` or `play`, and
  * its tree state is `dirty`, whatever its `gameOrigin` kind (file, database, database draft,
    `none`) and whether auto-save is on; or
  * it is file-backed and its file-freshness state (`src/state/fileFreshness.ts:151-157`) is
    `conflict` or `unavailable` — such a tab can hold the only copy of the game with
    `dirty === false` (`src/components/tabs/FileFreshnessGate.tsx:250-261`), which is the
    MANDATE's "Save my version as a new game" tree; or
  * its tree cannot be inspected (tree-storage status `unreadable` or `unavailable`, or the
    inspection fails), because "could not check" is not "saved".

`puzzles` and `new` tabs never block (Not part of this task). The scan covers every workspace tab,
mounted or not, and is **read-only**:

* A tab with a cached tree store (`getCachedTreeStore(tab.value)`, `src/state/store/tree.ts:147`)
  is read from that store — it is authoritative, including in-memory edits — and the store is left
  in place: the scan never calls `closeTreeStore` (which also removes file freshness) and never
  calls `createTreeStore`.
* A tab without a cached store is read from the tab-storage repository at `getTabTreeKey(tab)`
  (`src/state/workspaceTypes.ts:31`), never at `tab.value`, through a read-only inspection that
  sees a pending frame first, keeps `unreadable`/`unavailable` distinct from absent, performs no
  migration write (`tabStorage.ts:496-501` is the migrating path) and does not change the
  repository's read status. If no such method exists, Phase 1 adds it to `TabStorageRepository`
  beside `readTree`, sharing `readTreeValue`.

The predicate and the scan live in one new module, `src/state/quitGate.ts`, beside the gate. It is
the quit gate's own predicate; the tab-close prompt's policy is f-20261101-14's to decide (Not part
of this task).

### O3 — The quit dialog

When the scan finds blocking tabs, one summary dialog opens (not `ConfirmChangesModal` per tab —
see Decisions):

* Title: the existing `Tab.UnsavedChanges` string. Body: a new message saying the listed tabs have
  unsaved changes that are lost on quit, followed by the tab names; an uninspectable tab carries a
  short "could not be checked" marker.
* **Cancel** (default focus; Escape and outside click map to it): closes the dialog, keeps the app
  running, navigates to the boards route (`/`) and activates the first listed tab, so the user
  lands where saving is needed, from whatever route the quit started (`/library`, `/settings`, …).
* **Quit without saving**: destroys the window directly (it does not re-enter the gate), with O1's
  destroy-failure fallback.
* **Scan error** (fail closed): the dialog opens with the generic message and no list, still offers
  both buttons, and Cancel keeps the current route and tab. The error is logged with the failing
  tab id through the renderer's existing warn/log route (`warnSafely`, `@/platform/errors`); no
  toast beyond the dialog.
* No blocking tab: the gate destroys the window immediately with no dialog (the existing
  `verify:app` clean-close assertion depends on this).

The dialog component is `src/components/tabs/QuitConfirmModal.tsx`, built on `AppModal` like
`ConfirmChangesModal`, mounted once in the root route. New strings go through i18next with non-empty
values in all 16 catalogues (`pnpm i18n:extract`, `pnpm i18n:check`).

### O4 — Capability

`src-tauri/capabilities/main.json` gains exactly `core:window:allow-destroy`. It is required: once a
JS close-request listener exists Tauri prevents the native close (`tauri-2.10.2`
`manager/window.rs:167-170`), and the only way to close the window afterwards is
`plugin:window|destroy`, which skips the close request. It grants destroying the app's own window
and nothing else; `allow-close` alone cannot do this (it re-raises the close request).

### O5 — Verification

Each item names the revert it must catch.

* Unit tests (`src/state/quitGate.test.ts`): the distinguishing predicate cases of O2 — dirty per
  origin kind (file, database, draft, `none`), clean, freshness `conflict`/`unavailable` with
  `dirty === false`, `unreadable`/`unavailable` storage, an inspection that throws, puzzles/new
  never block. Writer/reader agreement: after a scan, `getCachedTreeStore(id)` returns the same
  object, `sessionStorage` bytes are unchanged (including a legacy uncompressed tree), file
  freshness is unchanged, and a tab whose `getTabTreeKey(tab) !== tab.value` is read at its tree
  key. Gate decisions: clean → destroy once; blocking → no destroy, dialog requested; scan throw →
  no destroy, fail-closed dialog; destroy rejection → notify then `exit(0)`; a second entry while
  running or open → no second scan or dialog.
* Listener tests: the handler calls `preventDefault()` on every delivered event before its first
  await (a second delivery while the dialog is open, a scan throw, a gate exception), exactly one
  registration, unlisten on unmount and on unmount-before-settle, and the registration-failure
  notification.
* Menu tests (`src/routes/-appMenu.test.ts`): the macOS app-menu Quit is an app-defined item with
  `cmd+q` bound to the exit callback, and no predefined `Quit` item remains.
* Component test (`src/components/tabs/QuitConfirmModal.test.tsx`): names listed, Cancel navigates
  to `/` and activates the first listed tab, fail-closed Cancel keeps route and tab, Quit without
  saving destroys exactly once.
* Mocked-IPC Playwright. The fixture (`e2e/fixtures.ts`) gains the production pair, not a shortcut:
  `plugin:window|close` emits `tauri://close-requested` to the registered window listener, and
  `plugin:window|destroy` is recorded with no further emit. A new `quit-gate` spec: with a dirty tab,
  the title-bar × and the compact File → Exit menu each show the dialog (committed snapshot) and
  record no destroy, then Quit without saving records exactly one destroy; with no dirty tab, both
  record one destroy and no dialog. The existing `e2e/async-errors.spec.ts` File → Exit cases
  (`:678-715`, `:727-778`) move from `plugin:process|exit` to `plugin:window|destroy`. This is the
  proof that File → Exit no longer calls `exit(0)`: reverting `__root.tsx` turns both specs red.
* Real app (`pnpm verify:app`), mandatory: the existing clean close still exits. A dirty-tab
  scenario, using the harness's existing renderer-side tree edit (`scripts/verify-app.mjs:3249-3321`
  dirties a tree without native chrome), clicks the title-bar close and asserts the process stays
  alive with the dialog visible, then clicks Quit without saving and asserts exit with no child
  left; a restart under the same throwaway `HOME` asserts the dirty tab is not restored (the
  MANDATE's probe). The scenario runs in its own app session, or as the last close of its process,
  so no other `closeApplicationThroughTitlebar` site (`scripts/verify-app.mjs:857,3712,4035,4322,4329,4602,5803,5883,5917`)
  meets a dirty tab.

## Decisions and trade-offs

Decided autonomously (full auto):

* **D1 — One gate function, two entry points:** the close-request listener and the menu actions
  both call the same gate. Rejected: routing File → Exit and macOS Quit through `window.close()`
  so the listener is the only entry (r1 version) — the menu paths would then be ungated whenever
  listener registration failed (r1 review, I12). Rejected: a Rust-side
  `CloseRequested`/`ExitRequested` prevent that asks the renderer over a new IPC event (a new event
  and round trip for a decision only the renderer can make). Reversal: route the menu actions back
  to `exit(0)` and keep the listener.
* **D2 — Tab states that need a decision:** dirty `analysis`/`play` trees of every origin, file
  tabs in freshness `conflict`/`unavailable`, and uninspectable trees; puzzles and new tabs never
  block. Rejected: only persistent origins (the `closeTab` predicate) — the MANDATE names
  origin-`none` boards and database drafts. Rejected: ignoring uninspectable trees — "could not
  check" is not "saved" under a data-loss impact. Reversal: narrow the predicate in
  `src/state/quitGate.ts`.
* **D3 — Summary dialog, not per-tab `ConfirmChangesModal`:** one dialog with Cancel / Quit without
  saving. Rejected: chaining `ConfirmChangesModal` per dirty tab — it is bound to closing one tab
  (its `onSaved` closes the tab, its copy says "before closing"), n tabs means n dialogs, and
  origin-`none`/draft saves need their own picker flows mid-quit. Saving stays on the normal per-tab
  save surfaces after Cancel. Precedent: the existing unsaved-changes prompt on tab close
  (`BoardsPage.tsx:133`, `Tab.UnsavedChanges`) — the same promise extended to quitting, so this is
  not a novel product question. Reversal: add a "Save all" action later without changing the gate.
* **D4 — Fail closed with a way out:** every keep-alive path prevents the close, a scan error still
  shows the dialog with both buttons, and a failed destroy falls back to `exit(0)`. Rejected: fail
  open (silent data loss on a bug); rejected: block quit entirely (an app that cannot be closed).

## Risks / open questions

* **Session-storage premise not yet re-measured.** Planner mode cannot run `pnpm verify:app`. The
  2026-09-22 measurement and WebKitGTK's in-memory session storage both say trees do not survive a
  restart; Phase 2's restart assertion re-measures it. If it shows trees *do* survive, the gate is
  still correct for file/database tabs whose saved copy is older, but the dialog copy ("lost on
  quit") would be wrong — stop and return to planning rather than ship that copy.
* macOS and Windows cannot be run on this machine. The macOS Quit routing is proven by the menu unit
  test and the Traced premises; native confirmation on macOS/Windows is not observed here.
* Dock-menu Quit and OS logout on macOS go through the native terminate path and stay outside the
  gate (known limit; tao 0.34.5 exposes no `applicationShouldTerminate` hook).
* If listener registration fails, native window close is ungated for that session (notified; menu
  paths stay gated).

## Not part of this task

* The tab-close prompt's policy (origin-`none` dirty tab closes without a prompt): f-20261101-14, a
  co-located finding worked by the adopting session after the phases.
* f-20261101-18 (Elo `?` parsing).
* Persisting tabs across restarts, a "Save all" action, or saving from inside the quit dialog.
* Puzzle session lists that live in `sessionStorage` (not user-authored tree edits).
* Any change to `ExitGuard`, backend teardown, or the `beforeunload`/`pagehide` flush.

## Phases

### Phase 1 — Gate, predicate, dialog, menu routing, capability

* Obligations: O1, O2, O3, O4, and O5's unit, listener, menu and component tests.
* Files: `src/state/quitGate.ts` and `src/state/quitGate.test.ts` (new),
  `src/state/store/tabStorage.ts` (read-only inspection, only if absent), `src/routes/__root.tsx`,
  `src/routes/-appMenu.ts`, `src/routes/-appMenu.test.ts`,
  `src/components/tabs/QuitConfirmModal.tsx` and `src/components/tabs/QuitConfirmModal.test.tsx`
  (new), `src-tauri/capabilities/main.json`, `src/translation/*.json` (16 catalogues).
* Sensitive: persistence reads (`src/state/**`), listener lifetime, a capability change. Role:
  `sensitive`.
* PROOF COMMAND: `pnpm vitest run src/state src/routes src/components/tabs src/components/TopBar.window.test.ts && pnpm i18n:extract && pnpm i18n:check && pnpm lint`

### Phase 2 — Real-app and e2e proof

* Obligations: O5's Playwright fixture, spec and async-errors update, and the `verify:app`
  scenario including the restart probe.
* Files: `e2e/fixtures.ts`, `e2e/quit-gate.spec.ts` with committed snapshots (new), a `quit-gate`
  project in `playwright.config.ts`, `e2e/async-errors.spec.ts`, `scripts/verify-app.mjs`.
* Role: `normal`.
* PROOF COMMAND: `pnpm test:e2e:container --project=quit-gate --project=async-errors && pnpm build && pnpm verify:app`

## Reviews

### Round 1 (r1, 2026-11-01, 508 s wall, 12 lenses on Grok via leaf-launch, all profile checks 0)

Verdicts: plan REVISE (4), minimalism APPROVED (2), correctness REVISE (4), error-handling REVISE (4),
tests REVISE (4), ipc-contract REVISE (6), persisted-state REVISE (4); chess-semantics, pgn-index,
tauri-security NOT APPLICABLE + APPROVED; engine-protocol, platform-semantics APPROVED with no findings.
Raw reports: `$RUN_TMP/lens-<lens>-r1.txt`. Every finding below adopted as Fix in r2 after source
verification (window.js:1632-1640, useTauriListener.ts:37-45, tree.ts:147,237-243,821-822,
tabStorage.ts:449-501, FileFreshnessGate.tsx:250-261, async-errors.spec.ts:678-700,
verify-app.mjs:3249-3321). Adopted at r1: 28 findings → 20 issues.

* **I1** keep-alive paths must `preventDefault()` on every event before any await (second close, scan throw, gate exception) — witnesses correctness#1, error-handling#1, ipc-contract#1, ipc-contract#5 — MANDATE "the app quits at once and the edits are gone" — Fix: O1 handler prevents synchronously, gate destroys itself.
* **I2** `useTauriListener` cannot carry the close listener (fire-and-forget callback) — ipc-contract#2 — Fix: O1 names the awaited-handler requirement.
* **I3** File → Exit change breaks `e2e/async-errors.spec.ts` consumers outside Phase 2 proof — plan#1, ipc-contract#4 — Fix: Phase 2 files/proof include async-errors, assertions move to `plugin:window|destroy`.
* **I4** Cancel from a non-board route leaves the user off the save surfaces — plan#2 — Fix: Cancel navigates to `/` and activates the first listed tab.
* **I5** Phase 1 proof would not run the dialog test — plan#3, tests#3 — Fix: dialog pinned to `src/components/tabs/QuitConfirmModal.tsx`, proof paths cover `src/state`.
* **I6** dirty `verify:app` probe would break later titlebar closes — plan#4 — Fix: own session or last close of its process.
* **I7** shared-predicate justification is a seam for a future caller; sibling-module option unpinned — minimalism#1 — Fix: quit-only predicate pinned in `src/state/quitGate.ts`, future-caller justification removed.
* **I8** full cartesian unit matrix — minimalism#2 (nit) — Fix: distinguishing cases only (arbiter-closed nit).
* **I9** scan reader: cached store authoritative, never `closeTreeStore`/`createTreeStore`, read at tree key — correctness#2, persisted-state#1 — Fix: O2 reader contract.
* **I10** fail-closed Cancel has no tab to activate — correctness#3 — Fix: fail-closed Cancel keeps route and tab.
* **I11** Playwright fixture must emit `tauri://close-requested` on `close` and record `destroy` — correctness#4, tests#4, ipc-contract#3 — Fix: mandatory fixture pair in O5.
* **I12** listener registration failure leaves menu quits ungated — error-handling#2 — Fix: D1 revised, menu paths call the gate directly; registration failure notified.
* **I13** `destroy()` rejection has no surface or fallback — error-handling#3 — Fix: notify and fall back to `exit(0)` inside the gate.
* **I14** scan-throw cause dropped — error-handling#4 — Fix: logged with tab id via `warnSafely`.
* **I15** real-app dirty-close assertion was optional — tests#1 — Fix: mandatory, uses the harness's existing tree edit.
* **I16** File → Exit revert not caught by any proof — tests#2 — Fix: quit-gate spec covers compact File → Exit; async-errors asserts destroy.
* **I17** O4's "allow-close already permits in effect" is false — ipc-contract#6 (nit) — Fix: wording corrected.
* **I18** scan via `readTree` migrates (writes) legacy trees — persisted-state#2 — Fix: read-only inspection, no migration write, no status change.
* **I19** freshness `conflict`/`unavailable` tab with `dirty === false` holds the only copy — persisted-state#3 — Fix: O2 blocks on those states.
* **I20** tests do not prove writer/reader agreement — persisted-state#4 — Fix: O5 unit assertions (same store object, unchanged bytes and freshness, tree-key read).

### plan-r3.md

# Plan: one quit gate for unsaved tabs (f-20261101-13)

## Goal

Quitting ChessFable — title-bar close, native window close (decoration ×, Alt+F4, window-manager
close), File → Exit, and macOS Quit / Cmd+Q — never discards a tab with unsaved tree edits without
the user first confirming. A quit with nothing unsaved stays immediate.

## MANDATE

Verbatim from `tasks/findings.md`, f-20261101-13 (frontend-state, entry build, impact data-loss):

> **Where:** `src/routes/__root.tsx` (menu Exit is `exit(0)`), `src/components/TopBar.tsx` (title-bar close calls `windowControls.close()`), `src-tauri/src/main.rs` `app.run` (handles only `WindowEvent::Destroyed` and `ExitRequested`, `ExitGuard` sequences teardown only); the tab list (`src/state/atoms.ts:106,144`) and every tree (`src/state/store/tabStorage.ts:470-569`) live in `sessionStorage`; the only dirty check is `closeTab` in `src/components/tabs/BoardsPage.tsx:133`.
>
> **Defect:** no code listens for a close request (no `onCloseRequested` / `CloseRequested` anywhere in `src` or `src-tauri/src`). The ledger measured on 2026-09-22 (around line 12095) that this `sessionStorage` is cleared when the window closes, so the `beforeunload`/`pagehide` flush does not carry dirty trees across a quit.
>
> **Failure scenario:** with auto-save off, or with a tab auto-save does not cover (a file tab parked in the conflict/unavailable panel, a database draft, an origin-`none` board from paste, URL or FEN import), the user annotates and then closes the window or picks File → Exit: the app quits at once and the edits are gone. The conflict panel's "Save my version as a new game" tree is lost too.
>
> **Open question:** what does a single quit gate look like across window close, menu Exit and Cmd+Q (intercept `onCloseRequested` plus the menu path), which tab states count as needing a decision, and does it reuse `ConfirmChangesModal` per tab or one summary dialog? First confirm with a real-app probe (`pnpm verify:app`) that tab trees do not survive a restart.
>
> **Related:** f-20260831-16 rejected a `beforeunload` confirm only as the answer to flush-failure reporting, not for dirty tabs on quit.

## Threat model and non-goals

No adversary. The inputs are the user's own quit gestures and the app's own persisted tab state.
Environments: Linux (WebKitGTK, custom title bar or native GTK decorations), Windows (WebView2),
macOS (WKWebView, native app menu). A renderer that is hung or crashed cannot run any gate; that
is a known limit, not a target. A process kill (SIGKILL, power loss, OS logout that terminates the
process) is out of scope.

## Traced premises

Frozen at BASE `095048137821e5d7a03c6ae797f7e12153c0fa71`.

* `src/routes/__root.tsx:203` — menu callback `exit: () => exit(0)`; `exit` comes from
  `@/platform/native` (`src/platform/native.ts:16`, re-export of `@tauri-apps/plugin-process`).
  It is the only production `exit(` call in `src`.
* `tauri-plugin-process-2.3.1/src/commands.rs:8-9` — `exit` is `app.exit(code)`;
  `tauri-runtime-wry-2.10.0/src/lib.rs:4215-4227` — that becomes `RunEvent::ExitRequested
  { code: Some(code) }`, never a window `CloseRequested`. So a window close-request listener does
  not see File → Exit today.
* `src/components/TopBar.tsx:170-173,278` and `src/routes/-appMenu.ts:285-294` — the title-bar ×
  calls `appWindow.close()`. `tauri-runtime-wry-2.10.0/src/lib.rs:4168-4169` (native
  `TaoWindowEvent::CloseRequested`) and `:4231-4232` (`WindowMessage::Close`) both route to
  `on_close_requested` (`:4265-4290`), which closes unless a handler signals prevent.
* `tauri-2.10.2/src/manager/window.rs:167-170` — when the window has a JS listener for
  `tauri://close-requested`, Tauri prevents the close and emits the event to the window.
  `@tauri-apps/api@2.11.1` `window.js:1632-1640` — `onCloseRequested(handler)` awaits the
  handler and calls `this.destroy()` unless the handler called `preventDefault()`.
* `src-tauri/capabilities/main.json:22` grants `core:window:allow-close`; there is no
  `core:window:allow-destroy`, which `destroy()` needs.
* `src-tauri/src/main.rs:2457-2490` — `RunEvent::WindowEvent::Destroyed` cancels the window's
  operations; `ExitRequested` (both `code: None` after the last window is destroyed,
  `tauri-runtime-wry-2.10.0/src/lib.rs:4171-4185`, and `code: Some`) runs `ExitGuard` cleanup and
  then exits. Destroying the only window therefore reaches the same backend teardown as
  `exit(0)`.
* `src/routes/-appMenu.ts:87-97` — on macOS the application menu's Quit is the predefined
  `item: "Quit"` (muda `PredefinedMenuItemType::Quit`), whose native terminate path
  (`tao-0.34.5/src/platform_impl/macos/app_delegate.rs:63,133`, `applicationWillTerminate`)
  emits neither a window close request nor a renderer callback. `-appMenu.ts:132-140` — File →
  Exit is offered only off macOS.
* `src/components/tabs/BoardsPage.tsx:117-135` — `closeTab` asks only when
  `isPersistentGameOrigin(closedTab) && store.getState().dirty`; `src/utils/tabs.ts:65-68`
  `isPersistentGameOrigin` is false for origin `none`. `BoardsPage.tsx:302` renders tab panels
  with `keepMounted={false}`, so an inactive tab has no mounted tree store; `closeTab` reads one
  through `createTreeStore(value, undefined, getTabTreeKey(closedTab))` and disposes it.
* `src/state/store/tree.ts` — `dirty` is part of the persisted tree state, set by every editing
  action (`:388`, `:640-888`); `src/components/boards/BoardAnalysis.tsx:112-115` auto-saves only
  persistent, non-`databaseDraft` origins and only when `autoSaveAtom` is on.
* `src/state/store/tabStorage.ts:507-509` — per-tab tree status `not-read | available |
  unreadable | unavailable`; `:945-946` the `beforeunload`/`pagehide` best-effort flush
  (`d-20260901-14`: never throws, never notifies).
* `src/components/tabs/ConfirmChangesModal.tsx` — a single-tab dialog bound to tab close
  (`onSaved` closes the tab; `Tab.UnsavedChanges`, `Tab.UnsavedChangesConfirm` "…before
  closing?", `Tab.CloseWithoutSaving`).
* `scripts/verify-app.mjs:326-340,3976-3984` — `pnpm verify:app` exits the real app by clicking
  the title-bar close control and asserts the process exits; it must keep passing with no dirty
  tab.
* `docs/localization.md:3` — `pnpm i18n:check` requires every new key non-empty in all 16
  shipped catalogues under `src/translation/`.
* Session-storage premise: `tasks/findings.md` f-20260922-10 records "measured 2026-09-22 — the
  backing store is `sessionStorage` … which is cleared when the window closes". The MANDATE asks
  for a fresh real-app probe; planner mode cannot launch the app, so the probe is a Phase 2 proof
  obligation (see Risks).

## Approach

### O1 — One quit gate, reached from every quit gesture

One renderer function, the quit gate, decides every quit. It has two entry points and no other:

* **Close-request listener.** The root route registers exactly one `onCloseRequested` listener on
  the current window (obtained through `@/platform/native`, as `TopBar` does for `onResized`,
  `d-20260831-30`), with the matching unlisten on unmount, including an unmount that happens before
  the listen promise settles. The function handed to `onCloseRequested` is the function Tauri
  awaits; it is not wrapped in `useTauriListener`, whose fire-and-forget callback
  (`src/platform/useTauriListener.ts:37-45`) would let the wrapper destroy the window before the
  gate runs. **Every delivery calls `preventDefault()` on its own event synchronously, before any
  await**, and then runs the gate; the gate itself destroys the window when quitting is allowed.
  Consequently a second close request while the dialog is open or the scan is running, a scan
  error, and an exception anywhere in the gate all keep the window. This covers the title-bar ×,
  the native decoration ×, Alt+F4 and window-manager close (Traced premises).
* **Menu actions.** File → Exit stops calling `exit(0)` and calls the quit gate directly. On macOS
  the application menu's predefined `Quit` item is replaced by an app-defined item with the same
  label and the `cmd+q` shortcut whose action is the same direct call; the predefined item cannot
  be intercepted (Traced premises). Because the menu paths call the gate directly, they stay gated
  even if the close-request listener failed to register. The `exit` import leaves `__root.tsx`.

When quitting is allowed the gate destroys the window; the existing last-window `ExitRequested`
path in `main.rs` runs the unchanged backend teardown. No Rust change.

Failure contract:

* Listener registration rejects: notified once through the same `notifyUnlessCancelled` route
  `watchMaximized` uses (`src/routes/-appMenu.ts:498-512`), with its own message saying that closing
  the window will not ask about unsaved changes this session and that File → Exit (Cmd+Q on macOS)
  still does; also logged. Native window close is then ungated for that session (known limit).
* `destroy()` rejects (clean quit or Quit without saving): the error is notified and logged with a
  distinct destroy-fallback log line, and the gate falls back to `exit(0)` through
  `@/platform/native`, which reaches the same backend teardown, so the app can always be quit. The
  fallback lives inside the gate, not at the menu call sites.
* While one gate run is in progress or its dialog is open, a further entry from either entry point
  is a total no-op: no second scan, no second dialog, and no `destroy()` or `exit(0)` (the
  close-request entry has already prevented its event).
* The in-progress latch is released on every exit of a run — allowed quit, dialog Cancel, and any
  exception. An exception anywhere in a run after the latch is taken (not only in the scan) opens
  the fail-closed dialog of O3; only if opening that dialog itself fails does the gate release the
  latch and log, so the next entry starts a fresh run. A quit gesture therefore never meets a stuck
  latch.

### O2 — Which tabs need a decision

A tab blocks the quit when quitting would lose work that exists nowhere else:

* tab type `analysis` or `play`, and
  * its tree state is `dirty`, whatever its `gameOrigin` kind (file, database, database draft,
    `none`) and whether auto-save is on; or
  * it is file-backed and its file-freshness state (`src/state/fileFreshness.ts:151-157`) is
    `conflict` or `unavailable` — such a tab can hold the only copy of the game with
    `dirty === false` (`src/components/tabs/FileFreshnessGate.tsx:250-261`), which is the
    MANDATE's "Save my version as a new game" tree; or
  * its tree cannot be inspected (tree-storage status `unreadable` or `unavailable`, or the
    inspection fails), because "could not check" is not "saved".

`puzzles` and `new` tabs never block (Not part of this task). The scan covers every workspace tab,
mounted or not, and is **read-only**:

Keys: the tree store cache and file freshness are keyed by the logical owner `tab.value`
(`getCachedTreeStore`, `src/state/store/tree.ts:147`; `getFileFreshness(tab.value)`,
`src/state/fileFreshness.ts:204`, written under that owner by `FileFreshnessGate.tsx:146`); stored
tree bytes and their read status are keyed by `getTabTreeKey(tab)` (`src/state/workspaceTypes.ts:31`).
The scan uses exactly these keys, so a retargeted tab (`getTabTreeKey(tab) !== tab.value`) is read
correctly.

* A tab with a cached tree store is read from that store — it is authoritative, including in-memory
  edits — and the store is left in place: the scan never calls `closeTreeStore` (which also removes
  file freshness) and never calls `createTreeStore`. The uninspectable arm still applies: a cached
  tab whose `tabStorage.getStatus(getTabTreeKey(tab))` is `unreadable` or `unavailable` blocks even
  when its store says `dirty === false` (its store holds a default tree, `tabStorage.ts:443-445`),
  matching the refusal `closeTab` already makes (`BoardsPage.tsx:124-132`).
* A tab without a cached store is read from the tab-storage repository at `getTabTreeKey(tab)`,
  never at `tab.value`, through a read-only inspection that sees a pending frame first, keeps
  `unreadable`/`unavailable` distinct from absent, uses the production persist flag
  `requireExisting = getTabTreeKey(tab) !== tab.value` (`tree.ts:799`), performs no migration write
  (`tabStorage.ts:496-501` is the migrating path) and leaves `getStatus` unchanged (the shared
  `readTreeValue` calls `setReadStatus` on every path, `tabStorage.ts:460-503`, so the inspection
  must not reuse it unchanged). If no such method exists, Phase 1 adds it to
  `TabStorageRepository` beside `readTree`.

The predicate and the scan live in one new module, `src/state/quitGate.ts`, beside the gate. It is
the quit gate's own predicate; the tab-close prompt's policy is f-20261101-14's to decide (Not part
of this task).

### O3 — The quit dialog

When the scan finds blocking tabs, one summary dialog opens (not `ConfirmChangesModal` per tab —
see Decisions):

* Title: the existing `Tab.UnsavedChanges` string. Body: a new message saying the listed tabs have
  unsaved changes that are lost on quit, followed by the tab names; an uninspectable tab carries a
  short "could not be checked" marker.
* **Cancel** (default focus; Escape and outside click map to it): closes the dialog, keeps the app
  running, navigates to the boards route (`/`) and activates the first listed tab, so the user
  lands where saving is needed, from whatever route the quit started (`/library`, `/settings`, …).
* **Quit without saving**: destroys the window directly (it does not re-enter the gate), with O1's
  destroy-failure fallback.
* **Scan error** (fail closed): the dialog opens with the generic message and no list, still offers
  both buttons, and Cancel keeps the current route and tab. The error is logged with the failing
  tab id through the renderer's existing warn/log route (`warnSafely`, `@/platform/errors`); no
  toast beyond the dialog.
* No blocking tab: the gate destroys the window immediately with no dialog (the existing
  `verify:app` clean-close assertion depends on this).

The dialog component is `src/components/tabs/QuitConfirmModal.tsx`, built on `AppModal` like
`ConfirmChangesModal`, mounted once in the root route. New strings go through i18next with non-empty
values in all 16 catalogues (`pnpm i18n:extract`, `pnpm i18n:check`).

### O4 — Capability

`src-tauri/capabilities/main.json` gains exactly `core:window:allow-destroy`. It is required: once a
JS close-request listener exists Tauri prevents the native close (`tauri-2.10.2`
`manager/window.rs:167-170`), and the only way to close the window afterwards is
`plugin:window|destroy`, which skips the close request. It grants destroying the app's own window
and nothing else; `allow-close` alone cannot do this (it re-raises the close request).

### O5 — Verification

Each item names the revert it must catch.

* Unit tests (`src/state/quitGate.test.ts`): the distinguishing predicate cases of O2 — dirty per
  origin kind (file, database, draft, `none`), clean, freshness `conflict`/`unavailable` with
  `dirty === false`, `unreadable`/`unavailable` storage on both the cached and the uncached path, a
  never-mounted retargeted tab whose tree key is missing (`unavailable` under `requireExisting`), an
  inspection that throws, puzzles/new never block. Writer/reader agreement: after a scan,
  `getCachedTreeStore(id)` returns the same object, `sessionStorage` bytes are unchanged (including a
  legacy uncompressed tree), `tabStorage.getStatus` is unchanged for every tab (including a
  `not-read` one), file freshness is unchanged, and a retargeted tab in freshness `conflict` is
  found through `getFileFreshness(tab.value)` while its tree is read at its tree key. Gate
  decisions: clean → destroy once; blocking → no destroy, dialog requested; scan throw and a
  non-scan exception → no destroy, fail-closed dialog, latch released afterwards; destroy rejection
  → notify, fallback log line, then `exit(0)`; a second entry from either entry point while running
  or open → no second scan, no dialog, no destroy, no exit.
* Listener tests: the handler calls `preventDefault()` on every delivered event before its first
  await (a second delivery while the dialog is open, a scan throw, a gate exception), exactly one
  registration, unlisten on unmount and on unmount-before-settle, and the registration-failure
  notification with its specific message.
* Menu tests (`src/routes/-appMenu.test.ts`): the macOS app-menu Quit is an app-defined item with
  `cmd+q` bound to the exit callback, and no predefined `Quit` item remains.
* Component test (`src/components/tabs/QuitConfirmModal.test.tsx`): names listed, Cancel navigates
  to `/` and activates the first listed tab, fail-closed Cancel keeps route and tab, Quit without
  saving destroys exactly once.
* Mocked-IPC Playwright. The fixture (`e2e/fixtures.ts`) gains the production pair, not a shortcut:
  `plugin:window|close` emits `tauri://close-requested` to the registered window listener, and
  `plugin:window|destroy` is recorded with no further emit. A new `quit-gate` spec: with a dirty tab,
  the title-bar × and the compact File → Exit menu each show the dialog (committed snapshot) and
  record no destroy, then Quit without saving records exactly one destroy; with no dirty tab, both
  record one destroy and no dialog. The existing `e2e/async-errors.spec.ts` File → Exit cases
  (`:678-715`, `:727-778`) move from `plugin:process|exit` to `plugin:window|destroy` in Phase 1,
  the phase that changes File → Exit, so every phase stays green. This is the proof that File → Exit
  no longer calls `exit(0)`: reverting `__root.tsx` turns both specs red.
* Real app (`pnpm verify:app`), mandatory: the existing clean close still exits. A dirty-tab
  scenario, using the harness's existing renderer-side tree edit (`scripts/verify-app.mjs:3249-3321`
  dirties a tree without native chrome) in a session whose profile seeds `auto-save` to `false`
  the way the existing seed profile does (`scripts/verify-app.mjs:3691-3697`; `autoSaveAtom`
  defaults to true, `src/state/atoms.ts:507-511`, and would clear `dirty` on a file tab), asserts
  `tree.dirty === true` immediately before the close, clicks the title-bar close and asserts the process stays
  alive with the dialog visible, then clicks Quit without saving and asserts exit with no child
  left; a restart under the same throwaway `HOME` asserts the dirty tab is not restored (the
  MANDATE's probe). The scenario runs in its own app session, or as the last close of its process,
  so no other `closeApplicationThroughTitlebar` site (`scripts/verify-app.mjs:857,3712,4035,4322,4329,4602,5803,5883,5917`)
  meets a dirty tab. After both the clean and the confirmed quit, the app log of the throwaway
  `HOME` contains no destroy-fallback line — the proof that `core:window:allow-destroy` (O4) is in
  effect, since a missing grant would still exit through the `exit(0)` fallback.

## Decisions and trade-offs

Decided autonomously (full auto):

* **D1 — One gate function, two entry points:** the close-request listener and the menu actions
  both call the same gate. Rejected: routing File → Exit and macOS Quit through `window.close()`
  so the listener is the only entry (r1 version) — the menu paths would then be ungated whenever
  listener registration failed (r1 review, I12). Rejected: a Rust-side
  `CloseRequested`/`ExitRequested` prevent that asks the renderer over a new IPC event (a new event
  and round trip for a decision only the renderer can make). Reversal: route the menu actions back
  to `exit(0)` and keep the listener.
* **D2 — Tab states that need a decision:** dirty `analysis`/`play` trees of every origin, file
  tabs in freshness `conflict`/`unavailable`, and uninspectable trees; puzzles and new tabs never
  block. Rejected: only persistent origins (the `closeTab` predicate) — the MANDATE names
  origin-`none` boards and database drafts. Rejected: ignoring uninspectable trees — "could not
  check" is not "saved" under a data-loss impact. Reversal: narrow the predicate in
  `src/state/quitGate.ts`.
* **D3 — Summary dialog, not per-tab `ConfirmChangesModal`:** one dialog with Cancel / Quit without
  saving. Rejected: chaining `ConfirmChangesModal` per dirty tab — it is bound to closing one tab
  (its `onSaved` closes the tab, its copy says "before closing"), n tabs means n dialogs, and
  origin-`none`/draft saves need their own picker flows mid-quit. Saving stays on the normal per-tab
  save surfaces after Cancel. Precedent: the existing unsaved-changes prompt on tab close
  (`BoardsPage.tsx:133`, `Tab.UnsavedChanges`) — the same promise extended to quitting, so this is
  not a novel product question. Reversal: add a "Save all" action later without changing the gate.
* **D4 — Fail closed with a way out:** every keep-alive path prevents the close, a scan error still
  shows the dialog with both buttons, and a failed destroy falls back to `exit(0)`. Rejected: fail
  open (silent data loss on a bug); rejected: block quit entirely (an app that cannot be closed).

## Risks / open questions

* **Session-storage premise not yet re-measured.** Planner mode cannot run `pnpm verify:app`. The
  2026-09-22 measurement and WebKitGTK's in-memory session storage both say trees do not survive a
  restart; Phase 2's restart assertion re-measures it. If it shows trees *do* survive, the gate is
  still correct for file/database tabs whose saved copy is older, but the dialog copy ("lost on
  quit") would be wrong — stop and return to planning rather than ship that copy.
* macOS and Windows cannot be run on this machine. The macOS Quit routing is proven by the menu unit
  test and the Traced premises; native confirmation on macOS/Windows is not observed here.
* Dock-menu Quit and OS logout on macOS go through the native terminate path and stay outside the
  gate (known limit; tao 0.34.5 exposes no `applicationShouldTerminate` hook).
* If listener registration fails, native window close is ungated for that session (notified; menu
  paths stay gated).

## Not part of this task

* The tab-close prompt's policy (origin-`none` dirty tab closes without a prompt): f-20261101-14, a
  co-located finding worked by the adopting session after the phases.
* f-20261101-18 (Elo `?` parsing).
* Persisting tabs across restarts, a "Save all" action, or saving from inside the quit dialog.
* Puzzle session lists that live in `sessionStorage` (not user-authored tree edits).
* Any change to `ExitGuard`, backend teardown, or the `beforeunload`/`pagehide` flush.

## Phases

### Phase 1 — Gate, predicate, dialog, menu routing, capability

* Obligations: O1, O2, O3, O4, and O5's unit, listener, menu and component tests.
* Files: `src/state/quitGate.ts` and `src/state/quitGate.test.ts` (new),
  `src/state/store/tabStorage.ts` (read-only inspection, only if absent), `src/routes/__root.tsx`,
  `src/routes/-appMenu.ts`, `src/routes/-appMenu.test.ts`,
  `src/components/tabs/QuitConfirmModal.tsx` and `src/components/tabs/QuitConfirmModal.test.tsx`
  (new), `src-tauri/capabilities/main.json`, `src/translation/*.json` (16 catalogues),
  `e2e/async-errors.spec.ts` (File → Exit expectations and its `plugin:window|destroy` mock).
* Sensitive: persistence reads (`src/state/**`), listener lifetime, a capability change. Role:
  `sensitive`.
* PROOF COMMAND: `pnpm vitest run src/state src/routes src/components/tabs src/components/TopBar.window.test.ts && pnpm i18n:extract && pnpm i18n:check && pnpm lint && pnpm test:e2e:container --project=async-errors`

### Phase 2 — Real-app and e2e proof

* Obligations: O5's Playwright fixture and spec, and the `verify:app` scenario including the
  restart probe and the destroy-fallback log check.
* Files: `e2e/fixtures.ts`, `e2e/quit-gate.spec.ts` with committed snapshots (new), a `quit-gate`
  project in `playwright.config.ts`, `scripts/verify-app.mjs`.
* Role: `normal`.
* PROOF COMMAND: `pnpm test:e2e:container --project=quit-gate --project=async-errors && pnpm build && pnpm verify:app`

## Reviews

### Round 1 (r1, 2026-11-01, 508 s wall, 12 lenses on Grok via leaf-launch, all profile checks 0)

Verdicts: plan REVISE (4), minimalism APPROVED (2), correctness REVISE (4), error-handling REVISE (4),
tests REVISE (4), ipc-contract REVISE (6), persisted-state REVISE (4); chess-semantics, pgn-index,
tauri-security NOT APPLICABLE + APPROVED; engine-protocol, platform-semantics APPROVED with no findings.
Raw reports: `$RUN_TMP/lens-<lens>-r1.txt`. Every finding below adopted as Fix in r2 after source
verification (window.js:1632-1640, useTauriListener.ts:37-45, tree.ts:147,237-243,821-822,
tabStorage.ts:449-501, FileFreshnessGate.tsx:250-261, async-errors.spec.ts:678-700,
verify-app.mjs:3249-3321). Adopted at r1: 28 findings → 20 issues.

* **I1** keep-alive paths must `preventDefault()` on every event before any await (second close, scan throw, gate exception) — witnesses correctness#1, error-handling#1, ipc-contract#1, ipc-contract#5 — MANDATE "the app quits at once and the edits are gone" — Fix: O1 handler prevents synchronously, gate destroys itself.
* **I2** `useTauriListener` cannot carry the close listener (fire-and-forget callback) — ipc-contract#2 — Fix: O1 names the awaited-handler requirement.
* **I3** File → Exit change breaks `e2e/async-errors.spec.ts` consumers outside Phase 2 proof — plan#1, ipc-contract#4 — Fix: Phase 2 files/proof include async-errors, assertions move to `plugin:window|destroy`.
* **I4** Cancel from a non-board route leaves the user off the save surfaces — plan#2 — Fix: Cancel navigates to `/` and activates the first listed tab.
* **I5** Phase 1 proof would not run the dialog test — plan#3, tests#3 — Fix: dialog pinned to `src/components/tabs/QuitConfirmModal.tsx`, proof paths cover `src/state`.
* **I6** dirty `verify:app` probe would break later titlebar closes — plan#4 — Fix: own session or last close of its process.
* **I7** shared-predicate justification is a seam for a future caller; sibling-module option unpinned — minimalism#1 — Fix: quit-only predicate pinned in `src/state/quitGate.ts`, future-caller justification removed.
* **I8** full cartesian unit matrix — minimalism#2 (nit) — Fix: distinguishing cases only (arbiter-closed nit).
* **I9** scan reader: cached store authoritative, never `closeTreeStore`/`createTreeStore`, read at tree key — correctness#2, persisted-state#1 — Fix: O2 reader contract.
* **I10** fail-closed Cancel has no tab to activate — correctness#3 — Fix: fail-closed Cancel keeps route and tab.
* **I11** Playwright fixture must emit `tauri://close-requested` on `close` and record `destroy` — correctness#4, tests#4, ipc-contract#3 — Fix: mandatory fixture pair in O5.
* **I12** listener registration failure leaves menu quits ungated — error-handling#2 — Fix: D1 revised, menu paths call the gate directly; registration failure notified.
* **I13** `destroy()` rejection has no surface or fallback — error-handling#3 — Fix: notify and fall back to `exit(0)` inside the gate.
* **I14** scan-throw cause dropped — error-handling#4 — Fix: logged with tab id via `warnSafely`.
* **I15** real-app dirty-close assertion was optional — tests#1 — Fix: mandatory, uses the harness's existing tree edit.
* **I16** File → Exit revert not caught by any proof — tests#2 — Fix: quit-gate spec covers compact File → Exit; async-errors asserts destroy.
* **I17** O4's "allow-close already permits in effect" is false — ipc-contract#6 (nit) — Fix: wording corrected.
* **I18** scan via `readTree` migrates (writes) legacy trees — persisted-state#2 — Fix: read-only inspection, no migration write, no status change.
* **I19** freshness `conflict`/`unavailable` tab with `dirty === false` holds the only copy — persisted-state#3 — Fix: O2 blocks on those states.
* **I20** tests do not prove writer/reader agreement — persisted-state#4 — Fix: O5 unit assertions (same store object, unchanged bytes and freshness, tree-key read).

### plan-r4.md

# Plan: one quit gate for unsaved tabs (f-20261101-13)

## Goal

Quitting ChessFable — title-bar close, native window close (decoration ×, Alt+F4, window-manager
close), File → Exit, and macOS Quit / Cmd+Q — never discards a tab with unsaved tree edits without
the user first confirming. A quit with nothing unsaved stays immediate.

## MANDATE

Verbatim from `tasks/findings.md`, f-20261101-13 (frontend-state, entry build, impact data-loss):

> **Where:** `src/routes/__root.tsx` (menu Exit is `exit(0)`), `src/components/TopBar.tsx` (title-bar close calls `windowControls.close()`), `src-tauri/src/main.rs` `app.run` (handles only `WindowEvent::Destroyed` and `ExitRequested`, `ExitGuard` sequences teardown only); the tab list (`src/state/atoms.ts:106,144`) and every tree (`src/state/store/tabStorage.ts:470-569`) live in `sessionStorage`; the only dirty check is `closeTab` in `src/components/tabs/BoardsPage.tsx:133`.
>
> **Defect:** no code listens for a close request (no `onCloseRequested` / `CloseRequested` anywhere in `src` or `src-tauri/src`). The ledger measured on 2026-09-22 (around line 12095) that this `sessionStorage` is cleared when the window closes, so the `beforeunload`/`pagehide` flush does not carry dirty trees across a quit.
>
> **Failure scenario:** with auto-save off, or with a tab auto-save does not cover (a file tab parked in the conflict/unavailable panel, a database draft, an origin-`none` board from paste, URL or FEN import), the user annotates and then closes the window or picks File → Exit: the app quits at once and the edits are gone. The conflict panel's "Save my version as a new game" tree is lost too.
>
> **Open question:** what does a single quit gate look like across window close, menu Exit and Cmd+Q (intercept `onCloseRequested` plus the menu path), which tab states count as needing a decision, and does it reuse `ConfirmChangesModal` per tab or one summary dialog? First confirm with a real-app probe (`pnpm verify:app`) that tab trees do not survive a restart.
>
> **Related:** f-20260831-16 rejected a `beforeunload` confirm only as the answer to flush-failure reporting, not for dirty tabs on quit.

## Threat model and non-goals

No adversary. The inputs are the user's own quit gestures and the app's own persisted tab state.
Environments: Linux (WebKitGTK, custom title bar or native GTK decorations), Windows (WebView2),
macOS (WKWebView, native app menu). A renderer that is hung or crashed cannot run any gate; that
is a known limit, not a target. A process kill (SIGKILL, power loss, OS logout that terminates the
process) is out of scope.

## Traced premises

Frozen at BASE `095048137821e5d7a03c6ae797f7e12153c0fa71`.

* `src/routes/__root.tsx:203` — menu callback `exit: () => exit(0)`; `exit` comes from
  `@/platform/native` (`src/platform/native.ts:16`, re-export of `@tauri-apps/plugin-process`).
  It is the only production `exit(` call in `src`.
* `tauri-plugin-process-2.3.1/src/commands.rs:8-9` — `exit` is `app.exit(code)`;
  `tauri-runtime-wry-2.10.0/src/lib.rs:4215-4227` — that becomes `RunEvent::ExitRequested
  { code: Some(code) }`, never a window `CloseRequested`. So a window close-request listener does
  not see File → Exit today.
* `src/components/TopBar.tsx:170-173,278` and `src/routes/-appMenu.ts:285-294` — the title-bar ×
  calls `appWindow.close()`. `tauri-runtime-wry-2.10.0/src/lib.rs:4168-4169` (native
  `TaoWindowEvent::CloseRequested`) and `:4231-4232` (`WindowMessage::Close`) both route to
  `on_close_requested` (`:4265-4290`), which closes unless a handler signals prevent.
* `tauri-2.10.2/src/manager/window.rs:167-170` — when the window has a JS listener for
  `tauri://close-requested`, Tauri prevents the close and emits the event to the window.
  `@tauri-apps/api@2.11.1` `window.js:1632-1640` — `onCloseRequested(handler)` awaits the
  handler and calls `this.destroy()` unless the handler called `preventDefault()`.
* `src-tauri/capabilities/main.json:22` grants `core:window:allow-close`; there is no
  `core:window:allow-destroy`, which `destroy()` needs.
* `src-tauri/src/main.rs:2457-2490` — `RunEvent::WindowEvent::Destroyed` cancels the window's
  operations; `ExitRequested` (both `code: None` after the last window is destroyed,
  `tauri-runtime-wry-2.10.0/src/lib.rs:4171-4185`, and `code: Some`) runs `ExitGuard` cleanup and
  then exits. Destroying the only window therefore reaches the same backend teardown as
  `exit(0)`.
* `src/routes/-appMenu.ts:87-97` — on macOS the application menu's Quit is the predefined
  `item: "Quit"` (muda `PredefinedMenuItemType::Quit`), whose native terminate path
  (`tao-0.34.5/src/platform_impl/macos/app_delegate.rs:63,133`, `applicationWillTerminate`)
  emits neither a window close request nor a renderer callback. `-appMenu.ts:132-140` — File →
  Exit is offered only off macOS.
* `src/components/tabs/BoardsPage.tsx:117-135` — `closeTab` asks only when
  `isPersistentGameOrigin(closedTab) && store.getState().dirty`; `src/utils/tabs.ts:65-68`
  `isPersistentGameOrigin` is false for origin `none`. `BoardsPage.tsx:302` renders tab panels
  with `keepMounted={false}`, so an inactive tab has no mounted tree store; `closeTab` reads one
  through `createTreeStore(value, undefined, getTabTreeKey(closedTab))` and disposes it.
* `src/state/store/tree.ts` — `dirty` is part of the persisted tree state, set by every editing
  action (`:388`, `:640-888`); `src/components/boards/BoardAnalysis.tsx:112-115` auto-saves only
  persistent, non-`databaseDraft` origins and only when `autoSaveAtom` is on.
* `src/state/store/tabStorage.ts:507-509` — per-tab tree status `not-read | available |
  unreadable | unavailable`; `:945-946` the `beforeunload`/`pagehide` best-effort flush
  (`d-20260901-14`: never throws, never notifies).
* `src/components/tabs/ConfirmChangesModal.tsx` — a single-tab dialog bound to tab close
  (`onSaved` closes the tab; `Tab.UnsavedChanges`, `Tab.UnsavedChangesConfirm` "…before
  closing?", `Tab.CloseWithoutSaving`).
* `scripts/verify-app.mjs:326-340,3976-3984` — `pnpm verify:app` exits the real app by clicking
  the title-bar close control and asserts the process exits; it must keep passing with no dirty
  tab.
* `docs/localization.md:3` — `pnpm i18n:check` requires every new key non-empty in all 16
  shipped catalogues under `src/translation/`.
* Session-storage premise: `tasks/findings.md` f-20260922-10 records "measured 2026-09-22 — the
  backing store is `sessionStorage` … which is cleared when the window closes". The MANDATE asks
  for a fresh real-app probe; planner mode cannot launch the app, so the probe is a Phase 2 proof
  obligation (see Risks).

## Approach

### O1 — One quit gate, reached from every quit gesture

One renderer function, the quit gate, decides every quit. It has two entry points and no other:

* **Close-request listener.** The root route registers exactly one `onCloseRequested` listener on
  the current window (obtained through `@/platform/native`, as `TopBar` does for `onResized`,
  `d-20260831-30`), with the matching unlisten on unmount, including an unmount that happens before
  the listen promise settles. The function handed to `onCloseRequested` is the function Tauri
  awaits; it is not wrapped in `useTauriListener`, whose fire-and-forget callback
  (`src/platform/useTauriListener.ts:37-45`) would let the wrapper destroy the window before the
  gate runs. **Every delivery calls `preventDefault()` on its own event synchronously, before any
  await**, and then runs the gate; the gate itself destroys the window when quitting is allowed.
  Consequently a second close request while the dialog is open or the scan is running, a scan
  error, and an exception anywhere in the gate all keep the window. This covers the title-bar ×,
  the native decoration ×, Alt+F4 and window-manager close (Traced premises).
* **Menu actions.** File → Exit stops calling `exit(0)` and calls the quit gate directly. On macOS
  the application menu's predefined `Quit` item is replaced by an app-defined item with the same
  label and the `cmd+q` shortcut whose action is the same direct call; the predefined item cannot
  be intercepted (Traced premises). Because the menu paths call the gate directly, they stay gated
  even if the close-request listener failed to register. The `exit` import leaves `__root.tsx`.

When quitting is allowed the gate destroys the window; the existing last-window `ExitRequested`
path in `main.rs` runs the unchanged backend teardown. No Rust change.

Failure contract:

* Listener registration rejects: notified once through the same `notifyUnlessCancelled` route
  `watchMaximized` uses (`src/routes/-appMenu.ts:498-512`), with its own message saying that closing
  the window will not ask about unsaved changes this session and that File → Exit (Cmd+Q on macOS)
  still does; also logged. Native window close is then ungated for that session (known limit).
* `destroy()` rejects (clean quit or Quit without saving): the error is notified and logged, the log
  call is awaited (settled or rejected) so the diagnostic is handed to the native logger before the
  process ends, and the gate then falls back to `exit(0)` through `@/platform/native`, which reaches
  the same backend teardown, so the app can always be quit. The fallback lives inside the gate, not
  at the menu call sites.
* While one gate run is in progress or its dialog is open, a further entry from either entry point
  is a total no-op: no second scan, no second dialog, and no `destroy()` or `exit(0)` (the
  close-request entry has already prevented its event).
* The latch is held from the start of a run until the run ends, and an open dialog (normal or
  fail-closed) is part of the run. A run ends in exactly one of three ways, and only then is the
  latch released: the quit proceeds (`destroy()`, or its `exit(0)` fallback); the user cancels the
  dialog; or opening the fail-closed dialog itself fails, in which case the gate logs and releases
  so the next entry starts a fresh run. An exception anywhere in a run after the latch is taken
  (not only in the scan) opens the fail-closed dialog and keeps the latch held; no exception
  handler releases the latch while a dialog is open. A quit gesture therefore never meets a stuck
  latch and never runs beside an open dialog.

### O2 — Which tabs need a decision

A tab blocks the quit when quitting would lose work that exists nowhere else:

* tab type `analysis` or `play`, and
  * its tree state is `dirty`, whatever its `gameOrigin` kind (file, database, database draft,
    `none`) and whether auto-save is on; or
  * it is file-backed and its file-freshness state (`src/state/fileFreshness.ts:151-157`) is
    `conflict` or `unavailable` — such a tab can hold the only copy of the game with
    `dirty === false` (`src/components/tabs/FileFreshnessGate.tsx:250-261`), which is the
    MANDATE's "Save my version as a new game" tree; or
  * its tree cannot be inspected (tree-storage status `unreadable` or `unavailable`, or the
    inspection fails), because "could not check" is not "saved".

`puzzles` and `new` tabs never block (Not part of this task). The scan covers every workspace tab,
mounted or not, and is **read-only**:

Keys: the tree store cache and file freshness are keyed by the logical owner `tab.value`
(`getCachedTreeStore`, `src/state/store/tree.ts:147`; `getFileFreshness(tab.value)`,
`src/state/fileFreshness.ts:204`, written under that owner by `FileFreshnessGate.tsx:146`); stored
tree bytes and their read status are keyed by `getTabTreeKey(tab)` (`src/state/workspaceTypes.ts:31`).
The scan uses exactly these keys, so a retargeted tab (`getTabTreeKey(tab) !== tab.value`) is read
correctly.

* A tab with a cached tree store is read from that store — it is authoritative, including in-memory
  edits — and the store is left in place: the scan never calls `closeTreeStore` (which also removes
  file freshness) and never calls `createTreeStore`. The uninspectable arm still applies: a cached
  tab whose `tabStorage.getStatus(getTabTreeKey(tab))` is `unreadable` or `unavailable` blocks even
  when its store says `dirty === false` (its store holds a default tree, `tabStorage.ts:443-445`),
  matching the refusal `closeTab` already makes (`BoardsPage.tsx:124-132`).
* A tab without a cached store is read from the tab-storage repository at `getTabTreeKey(tab)`,
  never at `tab.value`, through a read-only inspection that sees a pending frame first, keeps
  `unreadable`/`unavailable` distinct from absent, uses the production persist flag
  `requireExisting = getTabTreeKey(tab) !== tab.value` (`tree.ts:799`), performs no migration write
  (`tabStorage.ts:496-501` is the migrating path) and leaves `getStatus` unchanged (the shared
  `readTreeValue` calls `setReadStatus` on every path, `tabStorage.ts:460-503`, so the inspection
  must not reuse it unchanged). If no such method exists, Phase 1 adds it to
  `TabStorageRepository` beside `readTree`.

The predicate and the scan live in one new module, `src/state/quitGate.ts`, beside the gate. It is
the quit gate's own predicate; the tab-close prompt's policy is f-20261101-14's to decide (Not part
of this task).

### O3 — The quit dialog

When the scan finds blocking tabs, one summary dialog opens (not `ConfirmChangesModal` per tab —
see Decisions):

* Title: the existing `Tab.UnsavedChanges` string. Body: a new message saying the listed tabs have
  unsaved changes that are lost on quit, followed by the tab names; an uninspectable tab carries a
  short "could not be checked" marker.
* **Cancel** (default focus; Escape and outside click map to it): closes the dialog, keeps the app
  running, navigates to the boards route (`/`) and activates the first listed tab, so the user
  lands where saving is needed, from whatever route the quit started (`/library`, `/settings`, …).
* **Quit without saving**: destroys the window directly (it does not re-enter the gate), with O1's
  destroy-failure fallback.
* **Scan error** (fail closed): the dialog opens with the generic message and no list, still offers
  both buttons, and Cancel keeps the current route and tab. The error is logged with the failing
  tab id through the renderer's existing warn/log route (`warnSafely`, `@/platform/errors`); no
  toast beyond the dialog.
* No blocking tab: the gate destroys the window immediately with no dialog (the existing
  `verify:app` clean-close assertion depends on this).

The dialog component is `src/components/tabs/QuitConfirmModal.tsx`, built on `AppModal` like
`ConfirmChangesModal`, mounted once in the root route. New strings go through i18next with non-empty
values in all 16 catalogues (`pnpm i18n:extract`, `pnpm i18n:check`).

### O4 — Capability

`src-tauri/capabilities/main.json` gains exactly `core:window:allow-destroy`. It is required: once a
JS close-request listener exists Tauri prevents the native close (`tauri-2.10.2`
`manager/window.rs:167-170`), and the only way to close the window afterwards is
`plugin:window|destroy`, which skips the close request. It grants destroying the app's own window
and nothing else; `allow-close` alone cannot do this (it re-raises the close request).

### O5 — Verification

Each item names the revert it must catch.

* Unit tests (`src/state/quitGate.test.ts`): the distinguishing predicate cases of O2 — dirty per
  origin kind (file, database, draft, `none`), clean, freshness `conflict`/`unavailable` with
  `dirty === false`, `unreadable`/`unavailable` storage on both the cached and the uncached path, a
  never-mounted retargeted tab whose tree key is missing (`unavailable` under `requireExisting`), an
  inspection that throws, puzzles/new never block. Writer/reader agreement: after a scan,
  `getCachedTreeStore(id)` returns the same object, `sessionStorage` bytes are unchanged (including a
  legacy uncompressed tree), `tabStorage.getStatus` is unchanged for every tab (including a
  `not-read` one), file freshness is unchanged, and a retargeted tab in freshness `conflict` is
  found through `getFileFreshness(tab.value)` while its tree is read at its tree key. Gate
  decisions: clean → destroy once; blocking → no destroy, dialog requested; scan throw and a
  non-scan exception → no destroy, fail-closed dialog, latch released afterwards; destroy rejection
  → notify, awaited log, then `exit(0)`; a second entry from either entry point while running
  or open → no second scan, no dialog, no destroy, no exit.
* Listener tests: the handler calls `preventDefault()` on every delivered event before its first
  await (a second delivery while the dialog is open, a scan throw, a gate exception), exactly one
  registration, unlisten on unmount and on unmount-before-settle, and the registration-failure
  notification with its specific message.
* Menu tests (`src/routes/-appMenu.test.ts`): the macOS app-menu Quit is an app-defined item with
  `cmd+q` bound to the exit callback, and no predefined `Quit` item remains.
* Component test (`src/components/tabs/QuitConfirmModal.test.tsx`): names listed, Cancel navigates
  to `/` and activates the first listed tab, fail-closed Cancel keeps route and tab, Quit without
  saving destroys exactly once.
* Mocked-IPC Playwright. The fixture (`e2e/fixtures.ts`) gains the production pair, not a shortcut:
  `plugin:window|close` emits `tauri://close-requested` to the registered window listener, and
  `plugin:window|destroy` is recorded with no further emit. A new `quit-gate` spec: with a dirty tab,
  the title-bar × and the compact File → Exit menu each show the dialog (committed snapshot) and
  record no destroy, then Quit without saving records exactly one destroy; with no dirty tab, both
  record one destroy and no dialog. The existing `e2e/async-errors.spec.ts` File → Exit cases
  (`:678-715`, `:727-778`) move from `plugin:process|exit` to `plugin:window|destroy` in Phase 1,
  the phase that changes File → Exit, so every phase stays green. This is the proof that File → Exit
  no longer calls `exit(0)`: reverting `__root.tsx` turns both specs red.
* Real app (`pnpm verify:app`), mandatory: the existing clean close still exits. A dirty-tab
  scenario, using the harness's existing renderer-side tree edit (`scripts/verify-app.mjs:3249-3321`
  dirties a tree without native chrome) in a session whose profile seeds `auto-save` to `false`
  the way the existing seed profile does (`scripts/verify-app.mjs:3691-3697`; `autoSaveAtom`
  defaults to true, `src/state/atoms.ts:507-511`, and would clear `dirty` on a file tab), asserts
  `tree.dirty === true` immediately before the close, clicks the title-bar close and asserts the process stays
  alive with the dialog visible, then clicks Quit without saving and asserts exit with no child
  left; a restart under the same throwaway `HOME` asserts the dirty tab is not restored (the
  MANDATE's probe). The scenario runs in its own app session, or as the last close of its process,
  so no other `closeApplicationThroughTitlebar` site (`scripts/verify-app.mjs:857,3712,4035,4322,4329,4602,5803,5883,5917`)
  meets a dirty tab.
* Capability (O4): a unit test reads `src-tauri/capabilities/main.json` the way
  `src/platform/fork-updater.test.ts` already does (`?raw` import) and asserts the main capability
  grants `core:window:allow-destroy`. This is O4's revert proof: the `exit(0)` fallback would hide a
  missing grant from every process-level check, and the mocked IPC never evaluates the ACL.

## Decisions and trade-offs

Decided autonomously (full auto):

* **D1 — One gate function, two entry points:** the close-request listener and the menu actions
  both call the same gate. Rejected: routing File → Exit and macOS Quit through `window.close()`
  so the listener is the only entry (r1 version) — the menu paths would then be ungated whenever
  listener registration failed (r1 review, I12). Rejected: a Rust-side
  `CloseRequested`/`ExitRequested` prevent that asks the renderer over a new IPC event (a new event
  and round trip for a decision only the renderer can make). Reversal: route the menu actions back
  to `exit(0)` and keep the listener.
* **D2 — Tab states that need a decision:** dirty `analysis`/`play` trees of every origin, file
  tabs in freshness `conflict`/`unavailable`, and uninspectable trees; puzzles and new tabs never
  block. Rejected: only persistent origins (the `closeTab` predicate) — the MANDATE names
  origin-`none` boards and database drafts. Rejected: ignoring uninspectable trees — "could not
  check" is not "saved" under a data-loss impact. Reversal: narrow the predicate in
  `src/state/quitGate.ts`.
* **D3 — Summary dialog, not per-tab `ConfirmChangesModal`:** one dialog with Cancel / Quit without
  saving. Rejected: chaining `ConfirmChangesModal` per dirty tab — it is bound to closing one tab
  (its `onSaved` closes the tab, its copy says "before closing"), n tabs means n dialogs, and
  origin-`none`/draft saves need their own picker flows mid-quit. Saving stays on the normal per-tab
  save surfaces after Cancel. Precedent: the existing unsaved-changes prompt on tab close
  (`BoardsPage.tsx:133`, `Tab.UnsavedChanges`) — the same promise extended to quitting, so this is
  not a novel product question. Reversal: add a "Save all" action later without changing the gate.
* **D4 — Fail closed with a way out:** every keep-alive path prevents the close, a scan error still
  shows the dialog with both buttons, and a failed destroy falls back to `exit(0)`. Rejected: fail
  open (silent data loss on a bug); rejected: block quit entirely (an app that cannot be closed).

## Risks / open questions

* **Session-storage premise not yet re-measured.** Planner mode cannot run `pnpm verify:app`. The
  2026-09-22 measurement and WebKitGTK's in-memory session storage both say trees do not survive a
  restart; Phase 2's restart assertion re-measures it. If it shows trees *do* survive, the gate is
  still correct for file/database tabs whose saved copy is older, but the dialog copy ("lost on
  quit") would be wrong — stop and return to planning rather than ship that copy.
* macOS and Windows cannot be run on this machine. The macOS Quit routing is proven by the menu unit
  test and the Traced premises; native confirmation on macOS/Windows is not observed here.
* Dock-menu Quit and OS logout on macOS go through the native terminate path and stay outside the
  gate (known limit; tao 0.34.5 exposes no `applicationShouldTerminate` hook).
* If listener registration fails, native window close is ungated for that session (notified; menu
  paths stay gated).

## Not part of this task

* The tab-close prompt's policy (origin-`none` dirty tab closes without a prompt): f-20261101-14, a
  co-located finding worked by the adopting session after the phases.
* f-20261101-18 (Elo `?` parsing).
* Persisting tabs across restarts, a "Save all" action, or saving from inside the quit dialog.
* Puzzle session lists that live in `sessionStorage` (not user-authored tree edits).
* Any change to `ExitGuard`, backend teardown, or the `beforeunload`/`pagehide` flush.

## Phases

### Phase 1 — Gate, predicate, dialog, menu routing, capability

* Obligations: O1, O2, O3, O4, and O5's unit, listener, menu, component and capability tests.
* Files: `src/state/quitGate.ts` and `src/state/quitGate.test.ts` (new),
  `src/state/store/tabStorage.ts` (read-only inspection, only if absent), `src/routes/__root.tsx`,
